class SubdomainScanner:

text

def __init__(self):
    self.logger = get_logger("subdomain")
    socket.setdefaulttimeout(SOCKET_TIMEOUT)

# ==========================
# USER-AGENT ALÃ‰ATOIRE
# ==========================
def get_random_ua(self) -> str:
    return random.choice(USER_AGENTS)

# ==========================
# NETTOYAGE CIBLE
# ==========================
def clean_target(self, target: str) -> str:
    target = target.strip().lower()
    target = target.replace("https://", "").replace("http://", "")
    target = target.split("/")[0]
    target = target.split(":")[0]
    return target

# ==========================
# RÃ‰SOLUTION DNS
# ==========================
def resolve(self, subdomain: str) -> str | None:
    """
    Tente de rÃ©soudre un sous-domaine.
    Retourne l'IP si trouvÃ©, None sinon.
    """
    try:
        ip = socket.gethostbyname(subdomain)
        return ip
    except socket.gaierror:
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None

# ==========================
# VÃ‰RIF HTTP
# ==========================
def check_http(self, subdomain: str) -> dict:
    """
    VÃ©rifie si le sous-domaine rÃ©pond en HTTP/HTTPS.
    """
    for scheme in ("https", "http"):
        url = f"{scheme}://{subdomain}"
        try:
            response = requests.get(
                url,
                timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
                allow_redirects=True,
                headers={"User-Agent": self.get_random_ua()}
            )
            return {
                "url":    url,
                "status": response.status_code,
                "title":  self.extract_title(response.text),
                "server": response.headers.get("Server", "Unknown"),
            }
        except Exception:
            continue

    return {}

# ==========================
# EXTRACTION TITRE PAGE
# ==========================
def extract_title(self, html: str) -> str:
    """
    Extrait le titre de la page HTML.
    """
    import re
    match = re.search(
        r"<title[^>]*>(.*?)</title>",
        html,
        re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL
    )
    if match:
        title = match.group(1).strip()
        # Limite Ã  80 caractÃ¨res
        return title[:80]
    return "N/A"

# ==========================
# BRUTE FORCE WORDLIST
# ==========================
def bruteforce(
    self,
    domain: str,
    wordlist: list = None,
    check_http: bool = True,
    delay: float = 0.1
) -> list:
    """
    Tente de trouver des sous-domaines par brute force DNS.
    """
    if wordlist is None:
        wordlist = COMMON_SUBDOMAINS

    found = []
    total = len(wordlist)

    self.logger.info(
        "Bruteforce started for %s | wordlist=%s entries",
        domain, total
    )

    for i, word in enumerate(wordlist):
        subdomain = f"{word}.{domain}"

        ip = self.resolve(subdomain)

        if ip:
            entry = {
                "subdomain": subdomain,
                "ip":        ip,
                "http":      {},
            }

            # VÃ©rif HTTP optionnelle
            if check_http:
                entry["http"] = self.check_http(subdomain)

            found.append(entry)

            self.logger.info(
                "Found: %s -> %s", subdomain, ip
            )

        # Rate limiting lÃ©ger
        if delay > 0:
            time.sleep(delay)

    self.logger.info(
        "Bruteforce complete for %s | found=%s/%s",
        domain, len(found), total
    )

    return found

# ==========================
# SEARCH VIA CRT.SH
# ==========================
def search_crtsh(self, domain: str) -> list:
    """
    Recherche des sous-domaines via Certificate Transparency logs.
    Source : crt.sh (gratuit, sans clÃ© API)
    """
    url = f"https://crt.sh/?q=%.{domain}&output=json"
    found = []
    seen  = set()

    try:
        response = requests.get(
            url,
            timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
            headers={"User-Agent": self.get_random_ua()}
        )

        if response.status_code != 200:
            return []

        data = response.json()

        for entry in data:
            names = entry.get("name_value", "")
            for name in names.splitlines():
                name = name.strip().lower().lstrip("*.")
                if (
                    name
                    and domain in name
                    and name not in seen
                ):
                    seen.add(name)
                    found.append(name)

        self.logger.info(
            "crt.sh found %s subdomains for %s",
            len(found), domain
        )

    except requests.exceptions.Timeout:
        self.logger.warning("crt.sh timeout for %s", domain)

    except Exception as e:
        self.logger.warning(
            "crt.sh failed for %s: %s", domain, e
        )

    return sorted(found)

# ==========================
# ANALYSE PRINCIPALE
# ==========================
def analyze(self, target: str) -> dict:
    domain = self.clean_target(target)

    self.logger.info("Subdomain scan started for %s", domain)

    data = {
        "domain":     domain,
        "bruteforce": [],
        "crtsh":      [],
        "total":      0,
        "error":      None,
    }

    if not domain:
        data["error"] = "Domaine vide ou invalide"
        return data

    # Brute force DNS
    data["bruteforce"] = self.bruteforce(domain)

    # crt.sh CT logs
    data["crtsh"] = self.search_crtsh(domain)

    # Total unique
    all_found = set()
    for entry in data["bruteforce"]:
        all_found.add(entry["subdomain"])
    for sub in data["crtsh"]:
        all_found.add(sub)

    data["total"] = len(all_found)

    self.logger.info(
        "Subdomain scan complete for %s | total=%s",
        domain, data["total"]
    )

    return data

# ==========================
# DISPLAY
# ==========================
def display(self, data: dict) -> str:
    if data.get("error"):
        return f"""
â•”â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•—
â•‘ SUBDOMAIN ERROR â•‘

"""

    # Brute force display

    if bruteforce:

        crtsh_display = "  Aucun rÃ©sultat crt.sh\n"

â•”â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•—

â”€â”€ TARGET â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
Domain: {data.get("domain", "N/A")}

{bf_display}

PowerShell

cat modules\ports.py
cat modules\subdomain.py

🎯 Ajout des Cards WHOIS dans la GUI
Le module whois.py retourne des champs très structurés (registrar, organization, creation_date, expiration_date, emails, status, etc.). Nous allons ajouter un formateur de cartes HTML pour WHOIS dans gui/formatter.py.

1. Mettre à jour gui/formatter.py
Nous allons réécrire gui/formatter.py pour y ajouter la fonction format_whois ainsi que sa liaison dans l'aiguillage principal format_result.

Copie-colle cette commande dans PowerShell :

PowerShell

Set-Content -Path "gui\formatter.py" -Encoding UTF8 -Value @'
# ==========================
# gui/formatter.py
# Formateur de résultats en cards HTML
# ==========================

import json


def _yes_no(value):
    if value:
        return '<span style="color:#00ff66;">&#10003;</span>'
    return '<span style="color:#ff4444;">&#10007;</span>'


def _card(title, content, color="#ff2222"):
    return f"""
    <div style="
        background: #0a0a0a;
        border: 1px solid {color};
        border-radius: 4px;
        margin: 8px 0;
        padding: 0;
    ">
        <div style="
            background: {color}22;
            border-bottom: 1px solid {color};
            padding: 8px 12px;
            font-weight: bold;
            color: {color};
            font-size: 13px;
        ">{title}</div>
        <div style="
            padding: 10px 14px;
            color: #cccccc;
            font-size: 12px;
            line-height: 1.6;
        ">{content}</div>
    </div>
    """


def _row(label, value, label_color="#ff6666"):
    return f'<span style="color:{label_color};font-weight:bold;">{label}</span> : {value}<br>'


def _list_items(items):
    if not items:
        return '<span style="color:#666;">aucun</span>'
    return "<br>".join(f'&nbsp;&nbsp;&#8226; {item}' for item in items)


def _badge(text, color="#00ff66"):
    return f'<span style="background:{color}22;color:{color};padding:2px 8px;border:1px solid {color};border-radius:3px;font-size:11px;">{text}</span>&nbsp;'


def format_dns(data):
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            data = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data

    domain = data.get("domain", "?")
    summary = data.get("summary", {})

    badges = ""
    badges += _badge("IPv4", "#00ff66") if summary.get("has_ipv4") else _badge("IPv4", "#ff4444")
    badges += _badge("IPv6", "#00ff66") if summary.get("has_ipv6") else _badge("IPv6", "#ff4444")
    badges += _badge("Mail", "#00ff66") if summary.get("has_mail") else _badge("Mail", "#ff4444")
    if summary.get("cloudflare"):
        badges += _badge("Cloudflare", "#ffaa00")

    overview = _row("Domain", domain)
    overview += _row("IPs", str(summary.get("ip_count", 0)))
    overview += _row("Nameservers", str(summary.get("nameservers", 0)))
    overview += f"<br>{badges}"

    security = ""
    security += _row("SPF", _yes_no(summary.get("has_spf")))
    security += _row("DMARC", _yes_no(summary.get("has_dmarc")))
    security += _row("DKIM", _yes_no(summary.get("has_dkim")))

    records_a = _list_items(data.get("A", []))
    records_aaaa = _list_items(data.get("AAAA", []))
    records_mx = _list_items(data.get("MX", []))
    records_ns = _list_items(data.get("NS", []))
    records_txt = _list_items(data.get("TXT", []))
    records_cname = _list_items(data.get("CNAME", []))
    records_soa = _list_items(data.get("SOA", []))
    records_rev = _list_items(data.get("reverse", []))

    html = f"""
    <div style="font-family:Consolas,monospace;">
        {_card("DNS SCAN &mdash; " + domain, overview, "#ff2222")}
        {_card("SECURITY", security, "#ffaa00")}
        {_card("A (IPv4)", records_a, "#00ff66")}
        {_card("AAAA (IPv6)", records_aaaa, "#00aaff")}
        {_card("MX (Mail)", records_mx, "#ff6600")}
        {_card("NS (Nameservers)", records_ns, "#aa66ff")}
        {_card("TXT", records_txt, "#666666")}
        {_card("CNAME", records_cname, "#666666")}
        {_card("SOA", records_soa, "#666666")}
        {_card("Reverse DNS", records_rev, "#666666")}
    </div>
    """
    return html


def format_ip(data):
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            data = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data

    ip = data.get("ip", "?")
    geo = data.get("geo", {})
    ip_type = data.get("ip_type", {})
    proxy = data.get("proxy_flags", {})

    badges = ""
    badges += _badge(ip_type.get("type", "?"), "#00ff66")
    if geo.get("hosting"):
        badges += _badge("Hosting", "#ffaa00")
    if geo.get("proxy"):
        badges += _badge("Proxy", "#ff4444")
    if geo.get("mobile"):
        badges += _badge("Mobile", "#00aaff")

    overview = _row("IP", ip)
    overview += _row("Reverse DNS", data.get("reverse_dns", "?"))
    overview += _row("Hostname", data.get("hostname", "?"))
    overview += f"<br>{badges}"

    location = ""
    location += _row("Country", f"{geo.get('country', '?')} ({geo.get('country_code', '?')})")
    location += _row("Region", geo.get("region", "?"))
    location += _row("City", geo.get("city", "?"))
    location += _row("ZIP", geo.get("zip", "?"))
    location += _row("Coords", f"{geo.get('lat', '?')}, {geo.get('lon', '?')}")
    location += _row("Timezone", geo.get("timezone", "?"))

    network = ""
    network += _row("ISP", geo.get("isp", "?"))
    network += _row("Org", geo.get("org", "?"))
    network += _row("ASN", geo.get("asn", "?"))

    flags = proxy.get("flags", [])
    flag_html = _list_items(flags) if flags else '<span style="color:#00ff66;">Clean</span>'

    html = f"""
    <div style="font-family:Consolas,monospace;">
        {_card("IP ANALYSIS &mdash; " + ip, overview, "#ff2222")}
        {_card("GEOLOCATION", location, "#00aaff")}
        {_card("NETWORK", network, "#aa66ff")}
        {_card("PROXY / FLAGS", flag_html, "#ffaa00")}
    </div>
    """
    return html


def format_headers(data):
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            data = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data

    url = data.get("url", "?")
    status = data.get("status", "?")
    server = data.get("server", "?")
    security = data.get("security", {})

    score = security.get("score", 0)
    max_score = security.get("max", 7)
    level = security.get("level", "?")

    if score >= 6:
        score_color = "#00ff66"
    elif score >= 4:
        score_color = "#ffaa00"
    else:
        score_color = "#ff4444"

    overview = _row("URL", url)
    overview += _row("Status", str(status))
    overview += _row("Server", server)
    overview += f'<br><span style="color:{score_color};font-size:18px;font-weight:bold;">{score}/{max_score}</span> &mdash; {level}'

    checks_html = ""
    checks = security.get("checks", {})
    for name, info in checks.items():
        present = info.get("present", False)
        icon = _yes_no(present)
        val = info.get("value", "")
        if val and len(str(val)) > 60:
            val = str(val)[:60] + "..."
        checks_html += f"{icon} <b>{name}</b>"
        if val:
            checks_html += f' : <span style="color:#999;">{val}</span>'
        checks_html += "<br>"

    leaking = security.get("leaking", {})
    leak_html = ""
    if leaking:
        for k, v in leaking.items():
            leak_html += _row(k, v, "#ff4444")
    else:
        leak_html = '<span style="color:#00ff66;">No leaking headers</span>'

    cookies = data.get("cookies", [])
    cookie_html = ""
    for c in cookies:
        name = c.get("name", "?")
        secure = _yes_no(c.get("secure"))
        http = _yes_no(c.get("httponly"))
        same = c.get("samesite", "?")
        cookie_html += f"<b>{name}</b> &mdash; Secure:{secure} HttpOnly:{http} SameSite:{same}<br>"
    if not cookie_html:
        cookie_html = '<span style="color:#666;">aucun</span>'

    html = f"""
    <div style="font-family:Consolas,monospace;">
        {_card("HEADERS &mdash; " + url, overview, "#ff2222")}
        {_card("SECURITY CHECKS", checks_html, "#ffaa00")}
        {_card("LEAKING HEADERS", leak_html, "#ff4444")}
        {_card("COOKIES", cookie_html, "#aa66ff")}
    </div>
    """
    return html


def format_whois(data):
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            data = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data

    domain = data.get("domain", "?")
    error = data.get("error")

    if error:
        return _card("WHOIS ERROR", f'<span style="color:#ff4444;font-weight:bold;">{error}</span>', "#ff4444")

    # Badges
    dnssec = data.get("dnssec", "Unknown")
    badges = ""
    if "signed" in str(dnssec).lower() or dnssec == True:
        badges += _badge("DNSSEC Signed", "#00ff66")
    else:
        badges += _badge("DNSSEC Unsigned", "#ff4444")

    # Overview
    overview = _row("Domain", domain)
    overview += _row("Registrar", data.get("registrar", "Unknown"))
    overview += _row("Organization", data.get("organization", "Unknown"))
    overview += _row("Country", data.get("country", "Unknown"))
    overview += f"<br>{badges}"

    # Dates
    dates_html = _row("Created", data.get("creation_date", "Unknown"))
    dates_html += _row("Updated", data.get("updated_date", "Unknown"))
    dates_html += _row("Expires", data.get("expiration_date", "Unknown"))

    # Lists
    ns_html = _list_items(data.get("name_servers", []))
    emails_html = _list_items(data.get("emails", []))
    status_html = _list_items(data.get("status", []))

    html = f"""
    <div style="font-family:Consolas,monospace;">
        {_card("WHOIS ANALYSIS &mdash; " + domain, overview, "#ff2222")}
        {_card("TIMESTAMPS", dates_html, "#ffaa00")}
        {_card("NAME SERVERS (NS)", ns_html, "#aa66ff")}
        {_card("CONTACT EMAILS", emails_html, "#00aaff")}
        {_card("DOMAIN STATUS", status_html, "#666666")}
    </div>
    """
    return html


def format_result(data, scan_type="unknown"):
    """Point d'entrée : formate selon le type de scan."""
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data
    else:
        parsed = data

    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
        return str(data)

    formatters = {
        "dns": format_dns,
        "ip": format_ip,
        "headers": format_headers,
        "whois": format_whois,
    }

    if scan_type in formatters:
        return formatters[scan_type](parsed)

    # Fallback : JSON indenté propre
    return f'<pre style="color:#00ff44;font-family:Consolas;font-size:12px;">{json.dumps(parsed, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}</pre>'
'@

tient le fichier : (venv) PS C:\Users\Administrateur\DED_SEC-OSINT> cat modules\whois.py

==========================
modules/whois.py
from datetime import date, datetime
from urllib.parse import urlparse
import ipaddress

import whois

from core.logger import get_logger

class WHOISAnalyzer:

text

def init(self):
self.logger = get_logger("whois")

==========================
NORMALISATION DU DOMAINE
==========================
def normalize_domain(self, target: str) -> str:
"""
Transforme une URL ou un domaine en domaine propre.

text

Exemples :
https://example.com/page -> example.com
example.com:8080          -> example.com
"""
target = str(target).strip().lower()

if not target:
    return ""

if not target.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
    target = "https://" + target

parsed = urlparse(target)
domain = parsed.hostname or ""

# Retire le point final parfois prÃ©sent dans un FQDN.
return domain.rstrip(".")
==========================
VALIDATION
==========================
def is_ip_address(self, value: str) -> bool:
try:
ipaddress.ip_address(value)
return True
except ValueError:
return False

==========================
NORMALISATION DES VALEURS
==========================
def normalize_value(self, value, default="Unknown"):
"""
Convertit les valeurs WHOIS en donnÃ©es faciles Ã afficher
et Ã sauvegarder en JSON.
"""
if value is None or value == "":
return default

text

if isinstance(value, (datetime, date)):
    return value.isoformat()

if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
    values = [
        self.normalize_value(item, default=None)
        for item in value
    ]
    return [item for item in values if item is not None]

return str(value)
def normalize_list(self, value) -> list:
"""Retourne toujours une liste sans doublons."""
if value is None:
return []

text

if not isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
    value = [value]

cleaned = []
seen = set()

for item in value:
    item = self.normalize_value(item, default=None)

    if item and item not in seen:
        seen.add(item)
        cleaned.append(item)

return cleaned
==========================
ANALYSE WHOIS
==========================
def analyze(self, target: str) -> dict:
domain = self.normalize_domain(target)

text

data = {
    "domain": domain,
    "registrar": "Unknown",
    "organization": "Unknown",
    "country": "Unknown",
    "creation_date": "Unknown",
    "expiration_date": "Unknown",
    "updated_date": "Unknown",
    "name_servers": [],
    "emails": [],
    "status": [],
    "dnssec": "Unknown",
    "error": None,
}

if not domain:
    data["error"] = "Domaine vide ou invalide"
    return data

# Le package python-whois n'est pas prÃ©vu pour analyser les IP.
# L'analyse IP est dÃ©jÃ  gÃ©rÃ©e par modules/ip.py.
if self.is_ip_address(domain):
    data["error"] = (
        "Cette cible est une adresse IP. "
        "Utilise le module IP Analyzer pour les IPs."
    )
    return data

self.logger.info("WHOIS lookup started for %s", domain)

try:
    result = whois.whois(domain)

    # Certains TLD retournent un objet vide plutÃ´t qu'une erreur.
    if not result:
        data["error"] = "Aucune donnÃ©e WHOIS disponible"
        return data

    data["registrar"] = self.normalize_value(
        result.get("registrar")
    )

    data["organization"] = self.normalize_value(
        result.get("org")
        or result.get("organization")
    )

    data["country"] = self.normalize_value(
        result.get("country")
    )

    data["creation_date"] = self.normalize_value(
        result.get("creation_date")
    )

    data["expiration_date"] = self.normalize_value(
        result.get("expiration_date")
    )

    data["updated_date"] = self.normalize_value(
        result.get("updated_date")
    )

    data["name_servers"] = self.normalize_list(
        result.get("name_servers")
    )

    data["emails"] = self.normalize_list(
        result.get("emails")
    )

    data["status"] = self.normalize_list(
        result.get("status")
    )

    data["dnssec"] = self.normalize_value(
        result.get("dnssec")
    )

    self.logger.info(
        "WHOIS lookup complete for %s | registrar=%s",
        domain,
        data["registrar"]
    )

except Exception as error:
    # Les erreurs WHOIS varient fortement suivant le TLD,
    # donc on log l'erreur exacte sans faire crash l'outil.
    self.logger.warning(
        "WHOIS lookup failed for %s: %s",
        domain,
        error
    )
    data["error"] = str(error)

return data

Thought for 7 seconds

Nous avons un problème d'indentation introduit par le patch. Les lignes avec self._current_scan_type = "dns" etc sont indentées de 16 espaces au lieu de 8. Il faut corriger. Plutôt que de refaire le patch entier, on peut faire un script python qui corrige les lignes problématiques. Il faut être précis._

Le fichier gui/window.py est affiché en entier. On voit les lignes comme :



    def run_dns(self):
        target = self.osint_input.text().strip()
        if not target:
            self.log("DNS ERROR : aucune cible")
            return
                self._current_scan_type = "dns"
        self.log(f"DNS scan → {target}")
Il y a bien une indentation excessive (16 espaces) pour ces lignes. On peut faire un remplacement ciblé avec regex : remplacer \n self._current_scan_type par \n self._current_scan_type (8 espaces). Mais il y a plusieurs occurrences avec 16 espaces exactement.

On peut faire un script Python qui lit le fichier, et pour chaque ligne, si elle commence par 16 espaces et contient self._current_scan_type, remplacer 16 par 8. C'est sûr car les autres lignes n'ont pas ce motif._

Aussi il y a des occurrences comme self._current_scan_type = "pkg" avec 16 espaces. Pareil._

Il faut aussi vérifier que les lignes de self.log après sont bien indentées à 8 espaces, ce qui est le cas. Donc en corrigeant l'indentation de ces lignes, le code devrait être valide.

On peut faire ça avec une commande PowerShell/py.

Alternative : puisque le patch a mal inséré les lignes (return\n self._current_scan_type...), on pourrait simplement réécrire les blocs de méthodes concernées, mais le script de correction est plus rapide._

Proposons :

powershell


python -c "
from pathlib import Path
path = Path('gui/window.py')
text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
lines = text.splitlines()
new_lines = []
for line in lines:
    if line.startswith('                self._current_scan_type'):
        line = '        self._current_scan_type' + line[20:]
    new_lines.append(line)
path.write_text('\n'.join(new_lines), encoding='utf-8')
print('OK')
"
Attention : line[20:] car 16 espaces = 16 chars, on veut 8, donc on enlève 8 premiers espaces ? En fait line commence par 16 espaces, on veut 8, donc on peut faire ' ' + line[16:] (8 espaces + le reste sans les 16 premiers). Mais il y a peut-être 16 espaces exactement. On peut calculer : on veut remplacer 16 espaces par 8, donc ' ' + line.lstrip() ? Non car line.lstrip() enlève tous les espaces, mais on veut garder exactement 8. Mieux : line[8:] si on enlève les 8 premiers espaces (car il y en a 16, en enlevant 8 il en reste 8). Oui, line[8:] enlève les 8 premiers caractères (qui sont des espaces), donc il reste 8 espaces + le code. C'est exact.

Donc :

python


for line in lines:
    if line.startswith('                self._current_scan_type'):  # 16 espaces
        line = line[8:]  # enlève 8 espaces, reste 8 espaces
Ça donne bien 8 espaces.

Il faut aussi vérifier les lignes self._current_scan_type = "pkg" etc._

Après correction, on compile.

On peut aussi faire un backup avant.

Ensuite, on lance la GUI pour tester.

L'utilisateur a déjà montré tout le fichier, donc on peut procéder.

Je vais lui donner la commande.

✅ Le problème est clair : les lignes self._current_scan_type ont 16 espaces au lieu de 8.
On corrige avec une commande simple.

1. Sauvegarder puis corriger

Copy-Item .\gui\window.py .\gui\window.py.patch-formatter.bak -Force

python -c "
from pathlib import Path
path = Path('gui/window.py')
text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
lines = text.splitlines()
new_lines = []
for line in lines:
    if line.startswith('                self._current_scan_type'):
        line = line[8:]  # retire 8 espaces, il en reste 8
    new_lines.append(line)
path.write_text('\n'.join(new_lines), encoding='utf-8')
print('[OK] Indentation corrigée')
"

inkling-medium



Thought for 5 seconds

Le problème est évident : le patch Python a inséré self._current_scan_type = "dns" et autres au mauvais endroit — après le return, dans le bloc if not target:._

Exemple :

python


        if not target:
            self.log("DNS ERROR : aucune cible")
            return
                self._current_scan_type = "dns"
        self.log(f"DNS scan → {target}")
Le self._current_scan_type est indenté sous le return, donc jamais exécuté, et le parser le voit comme un indent inattendu car il est au même niveau que self.log après return._

Il faut corriger toutes ces lignes en les mettant avant le self.log(...), pas après return. Et il faut aussi s'assurer qu'elles sont au bon niveau d'indentation.

Je vais donner une commande Python simple qui lit le fichier, corrige ces lignes spécifiques en supprimant celles qui sont après le return et en ajoutant au bon endroit.

Regardons le problème exact : le patch a remplacé chaque ligne self.log(...) par self._current_scan_type = ...\n self.log(...). Mais dans certains cas, il y avait un return avant le self.log(...), donc le remplacement a mis le _current_scan_type après le return, à un niveau d'indentation qui crée une erreur.

Solution simple : je vais supprimer toutes les lignes qui contiennent _current_scan_type dans le fichier actuel, et réinsérer proprement au bon endroit dans chaque méthode._

Alternativement, je peux utiliser sed/PowerShell pour corriger spécifiquement chaque méthode. Mais le plus sûr est de refaire le patch proprement avec un script qui sait où mettre chaque chose.

En fait, le plus rapide et le plus sûr est de simplement supprimer toutes les lignes mal placées (self._current_scan_type = ... qui sont après return) et d'ajouter au bon endroit — avant le self.log(...) dans chaque méthode._

Mais il y a une approche encore plus simple : utiliser python pour faire un remplacement global qui supprime toutes les lignes _current_scan_type du fichier actuel (elles sont toutes mal placées) et réinsère proprement dans chaque méthode au bon endroit._

Faisons cela avec une commande Python simple.

✅ Le problème est clair : le _current_scan_type a été inséré après le return, jamais exécuté, et le parser le voit comme indent erroné.
On va nettoyer toutes ces lignes mal placées et les remettre au bon endroit.

PowerShell

python -c "
import re
with open('gui/window.py', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    content = f.read()

# 1. Supprimer toutes les lignes _current_scan_type mal placées (celles après return)
content = re.sub(r'\\n                self\\._current_scan_type = .*', '', content)

# 2. Réinsérer proprement dans chaque méthode AVANT le self.log
replacements = {
    'self.log(f\"DNS scan': '        self._current_scan_type = \"dns\"\n        self.log(f\"DNS scan',
    'self.log(f\"IP analysis': '        self._current_scan_type = \"ip\"\n        self.log(f\"IP analysis',
    'self.log(f\"Headers analysis': '        self._current_scan_type = \"headers\"\n        self.log(f\"Headers analysis',
    'self.log(f\"WHOIS': '        self._current_scan_type = \"whois\"\n        self.log(f\"WHOIS',
    'self.log(f\"Full OSINT scan': '        self._current_scan_type = \"full\"\n        self.log(f\"Full OSINT scan',
    'self.log(f\"SSL analysis': '        self._current_scan_type = \"ssl\"\n        self.log(f\"SSL analysis',
    'self.log(f\"Subdomain scan': '        self._current_scan_type = \"subdomain\"\n        self.log(f\"Subdomain scan',
    'self.log(f\"Port scan': '        self._current_scan_type = \"ports\"\n        self.log(f\"Port scan',
    'self.log(f\"Domain analysis': '        self._current_scan_type = \"domain\"\n        self.log(f\"Domain analysis',
    'self.log(f\"Email analysis': '        self._current_scan_type = \"email\"\n        self.log(f\"Email analysis',
    'self.log(f\"Person search': '        self._current_scan_type = \"person\"\n        self.log(f\"Person search',
    'self.log(\"pkg update': '        self._current_scan_type = \"pkg\"\n        self.log(\"pkg update',
    'self.log(\"pkg upgrade': '        self._current_scan_type = \"pkg\"\n        self.log(\"pkg upgrade',
    'self.log(f\"pkg search': '        self._current_scan_type = \"pkg\"\n        self.log(f\"pkg search',
    'self.log(f\"pkg install': '        self._current_scan_type = \"pkg\"\n        self.log(f\"pkg install',
    'self.log(f\"pkg remove': '        self._current_scan_type = \"pkg\"\n        self.log(f\"pkg remove',
    'self.log(\"pkg list': '        self._current_scan_type = \"pkg\"\n        self.log(\"pkg list',
}

for old, new in replacements.items():
    content = content.replace(old, new, 1)

with open('gui/window.py', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    f.write(content)
print('Corrigé.')
"

        "font-size: 10px; padding: 5px;"
    )
    layout.addWidget(self.glitch_bottom)

    layout.addStretch(1)

    self.setLayout(layout)

    # ==========================
    # PHASES D'ANIMATION
    # ==========================
    self.phase = 0
    self.tick = 0
    self.logo_line_index = 0
    self.boot_msg_index = 0
    self.logo_lines = DED_SEC_LOGO.strip().split("\n")

    self.timer = QTimer(self)
    self.timer.timeout.connect(self.animate)

    QTimer.singleShot(300, self.start_animation)

def start_animation(self):
    self.phase = 1
    self.tick = 0
    self.timer.start(50)

def animate(self):
    self.tick += 1

    # PHASE 1 : GLITCH
    if self.phase == 1:
        glitch = "".join(
            random.choice(GLITCH_CHARS)
            for _ in range(random.randint(30, 60))
        )
        self.glitch_top.setText(glitch)

        glitch2 = "".join(
            random.choice(GLITCH_CHARS)
            for _ in range(random.randint(20, 50))
        )
        self.glitch_bottom.setText(glitch2)

        if self.tick > 20:
            self.phase = 2
            self.tick = 0
            self.logo_line_index = 0
            self.logo_opacity.setOpacity(1.0)

    # PHASE 2 : LOGO LIGNE PAR LIGNE
    elif self.phase == 2:
        if self.tick % 3 == 0:
            if self.logo_line_index < len(self.logo_lines):
                current = self.logo_label.text()
                new_line = self.logo_lines[self.logo_line_index]
                if current:
                    self.logo_label.setText(
                        current + "\n" + new_line
                    )
                else:
                    self.logo_label.setText(new_line)
                self.logo_line_index += 1
            else:
                self.phase = 3
                self.tick = 0

        if random.random() > 0.7:
            glitch = "".join(
                random.choice(GLITCH_CHARS)
                for _ in range(random.randint(10, 30))
            )
            self.glitch_top.setText(glitch)

    # PHASE 3 : SUBTITLE
    elif self.phase == 3:
        opacity = min(self.tick / 15.0, 1.0)
        self.sub_opacity.setOpacity(opacity)
        self.subtitle.setText("O S I N T    E N G I N E")

        if self.tick > 15:
            self.phase = 4
            self.tick = 0

    # PHASE 4 : BY DED_SEC
    elif self.phase == 4:
        opacity = min(self.tick / 15.0, 1.0)
        self.by_opacity.setOpacity(opacity)
        self.by_label.setText("by  DED_SEC")

        if self.tick > 15:
            self.phase = 5
            self.tick = 0
            self.log_opacity.setOpacity(1.0)

    # PHASE 5 : BOOT MESSAGES + PROGRESS
    elif self.phase == 5:
        total_msgs = len(BOOT_MESSAGES)
        progress_per_msg = 100.0 / total_msgs

        if self.tick % 6 == 0:
            if self.boot_msg_index < total_msgs:
                msg = BOOT_MESSAGES[self.boot_msg_index]

                if random.random() > 0.6:
                    glitch = "".join(
                        random.choice(GLITCH_CHARS)
                        for _ in range(4)
                    )
                    msg = f"{glitch} {msg}"

                self.boot_log.setText(msg)
                progress = int(
                    (self.boot_msg_index + 1) * progress_per_msg
                )
                self.progress.setValue(progress)
                self.boot_msg_index += 1
            else:
                self.boot_log.setText("█████ SYSTEM ONLINE █████")
                self.boot_log.setStyleSheet(
                    "color: #00ff66; font-family: Consolas; "
                    "font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold; "
                    "padding: 5px;"
                )
                self.progress.setValue(100)
                self.phase = 6
                self.tick = 0

        if random.random() > 0.8:
            glitch = "".join(
                random.choice(GLITCH_CHARS)
                for _ in range(random.randint(15, 40))
            )
            self.glitch_bottom.setText(glitch)

    # PHASE 6 : FONDU SORTIE
    elif self.phase == 6:
        opacity = max(1.0 - (self.tick / 20.0), 0.0)
        self.logo_opacity.setOpacity(opacity)
        self.sub_opacity.setOpacity(opacity)
        self.by_opacity.setOpacity(opacity)
        self.log_opacity.setOpacity(opacity)

        if self.tick > 25:
            self.timer.stop()
            self.boot_finished.emit()
            self.close()
==========================
WORKER THREAD
==========================
class ScanWorker(QThread):
result_ready = Signal(str)
log_ready = Signal(str)
finished_signal = Signal()

text

def __init__(self, task, *args, **kwargs):
    super().__init__()
    self.task = task
    self.args = args
    self.kwargs = kwargs

def run(self):
    try:
        result = self.task(*self.args, **self.kwargs)
        if isinstance(result, dict):
            self.result_ready.emit(
                json.dumps(
                    result, indent=2,
                    ensure_ascii=False, default=str
                )
            )
        else:
            self.result_ready.emit(str(result))
    except Exception as e:
        self.result_ready.emit(f"[ERREUR] {e}")
    finally:
        self.finished_signal.emit()

# ==========================
# LOG
# ==========================
def log(self, msg):
    self.logger.info(msg)
    glitch = random.choice(GLITCH_CHARS)
    self.console.append(f"  {glitch} [DED_SEC] {msg}")
    self.console.moveCursor(QTextCursor.End)

# ==========================
# CLEAR
# ==========================
def clear(self):
    self.results.clear()
    self.console.clear()
    self.console.append("  █ [SYSTEM] Console cleared")

def clear_person_fields(self):
    for field in [
        "pseudo_input", "firstname_input", "lastname_input",
        "city_input", "country_input", "age_input",
        "email_input", "phone_input"
    ]:
        getattr(self, field).clear()
    self.log("Person search fields cleared")

# ==========================
# WORKER
# ==========================
def run_worker(self, task, *args, **kwargs):
    if self.worker and self.worker.isRunning():
        self.log("⚠  Un scan est déjà en cours...")
        return
    self.set_buttons_enabled(False)
    self.status.setText("STATUS : SCANNING...")
    self.status.setStyleSheet(
        "color: #ff6600; font-weight: bold; font-size: 13px;"
    )
    self.progress.setVisible(True)
    self.worker = ScanWorker(task, *args, **kwargs)
    self.worker.result_ready.connect(self._display_result)
    self.worker.log_ready.connect(self.log)
    self.worker.finished_signal.connect(self.on_worker_done)
    self.worker.start()

def _display_result(self, raw_text):
    if hasattr(self, '_current_scan_type'):
        html = format_result(raw_text, self._current_scan_type)
        self.results.setHtml(html)
    else:
        self.results.append(raw_text)

def on_worker_done(self):
    self.status.setText("STATUS : ONLINE")
    self.status.setStyleSheet(
        "color: #00ff66; font-weight: bold; font-size: 13px;"
    )
    self.progress.setVisible(False)
    self.set_buttons_enabled(True)
    self.log("Scan terminé.")

def set_buttons_enabled(self, enabled):
    for btn in [
        self.btn_dns, self.btn_ip, self.btn_whois,
        self.btn_scan, self.btn_person,
        self.btn_headers, self.btn_ssl,
        self.btn_subdomain, self.btn_ports, self.btn_domain,
        self.btn_email
    ]:
        btn.setEnabled(enabled)

# ==========================
# PERSON SEARCH
# ==========================
def run_person_search(self):
    query = {
        "pseudo":     self.pseudo_input.text().strip(),
        "first_name": self.firstname_input.text().strip(),
        "last_name":  self.lastname_input.text().strip(),
        "city":       self.city_input.text().strip(),
        "country":    self.country_input.text().strip(),
        "age":        self.age_input.text().strip(),
        "email":      self.email_input.text().strip(),
        "phone":      self.phone_input.text().strip(),
    }
    has_input = any(
        v for v in query.values() if v and str(v).strip()
    )
    if not has_input:
        self.log("PERSON ERROR : remplis au moins un champ")
        return
    criteria = " | ".join(
        f"{k}={v}" for k, v in query.items()
        if v and str(v).strip()
    )
            self._current_scan_type = "person"
    self.log(f"Person search → {criteria}")
    self.run_worker(self.person_engine.search, query)

# ==========================
# PACKAGES
# ==========================
def _get_pkg_manager(self):
    from pkg.manager import PkgManager
    return PkgManager(sandbox_root=None)

def run_pkg_update(self):
            self._current_scan_type = "pkg"
    self.log("pkg update → GitHub Pages...")
    def task():
        import io, sys
        buf = io.StringIO()
        old = sys.stdout
        sys.stdout = buf
        self._get_pkg_manager().update()
        sys.stdout = old
        return buf.getvalue()
    self.run_worker(task)

def run_pkg_upgrade(self):
            self._current_scan_type = "pkg"

    def task():
        import io, sys
        buf = io.StringIO()
        old = sys.stdout
        sys.stdout = buf
        self._get_pkg_manager().upgrade()
        sys.stdout = old
        return buf.getvalue()
    self.run_worker(task)

# ==========================
# FENÊTRE SHELL INDÉPENDANTE
# ==========================
def open_shell(self):
    """Ouvre ou remet au premier plan la fenêtre du Shell."""
    if self.shell_window is None:
        self.shell_window = DedSecShellWindow(parent=self)
        self.shell_window.setAttribute(Qt.WA_DeleteOnClose, True)
        self.shell_window.destroyed.connect(
            lambda _obj=None: setattr(self, "shell_window", None)
        )

    self.shell_window.show()
    self.shell_window.raise_()
    self.shell_window.activateWindow()

# ==========================
# FERMETURE PROPRE
# ==========================
def closeEvent(self, event):
    if self.shell_window is not None:
        self.shell_window.close()
    super().closeEvent(event)

Le résultat dict est converti en JSON brut, puis .append() l'ajoute dans le QTextEdit.

Et dns.py a une méthode display() qui formate proprement... mais elle n'est jamais appelée dans la GUI. La GUI appelle analyze() qui retourne un dict.

Le plan

Modifier la zone RESULTS pour afficher du HTML au lieu du texte brut
Intercepter le résultat pour le formater en card
Étape 1 — Créer gui/formatter.py
PowerShell

Set-Content -Path "gui\formatter.py" -Encoding UTF8 -Value @'
# ==========================
# gui/formatter.py
# Formateur de résultats en cards HTML
# ==========================

import json


def _yes_no(value):
    if value:
        return '<span style="color:#00ff66;">&#10003;</span>'
    return '<span style="color:#ff4444;">&#10007;</span>'


def _card(title, content, color="#ff2222"):
    return f"""
    <div style="
        background: #0a0a0a;
        border: 1px solid {color};
        border-radius: 4px;
        margin: 8px 0;
        padding: 0;
    ">
        <div style="
            background: {color}22;
            border-bottom: 1px solid {color};
            padding: 8px 12px;
            font-weight: bold;
            color: {color};
            font-size: 13px;
        ">{title}</div>
        <div style="
            padding: 10px 14px;
            color: #cccccc;
            font-size: 12px;
            line-height: 1.6;
        ">{content}</div>
    </div>
    """


def _row(label, value, label_color="#ff6666"):
    return f'<span style="color:{label_color};font-weight:bold;">{label}</span> : {value}<br>'


def _list_items(items):
    if not items:
        return '<span style="color:#666;">aucun</span>'
    return "<br>".join(f'&nbsp;&nbsp;&#8226; {item}' for item in items)


def _badge(text, color="#00ff66"):
    return f'<span style="background:{color}22;color:{color};padding:2px 8px;border:1px solid {color};border-radius:3px;font-size:11px;">{text}</span>&nbsp;'


def format_dns(data):
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            data = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data

    domain = data.get("domain", "?")
    summary = data.get("summary", {})

    badges = ""
    badges += _badge("IPv4", "#00ff66") if summary.get("has_ipv4") else _badge("IPv4", "#ff4444")
    badges += _badge("IPv6", "#00ff66") if summary.get("has_ipv6") else _badge("IPv6", "#ff4444")
    badges += _badge("Mail", "#00ff66") if summary.get("has_mail") else _badge("Mail", "#ff4444")
    if summary.get("cloudflare"):
        badges += _badge("Cloudflare", "#ffaa00")

    overview = _row("Domain", domain)
    overview += _row("IPs", str(summary.get("ip_count", 0)))
    overview += _row("Nameservers", str(summary.get("nameservers", 0)))
    overview += f"<br>{badges}"

    security = ""
    security += _row("SPF", _yes_no(summary.get("has_spf")))
    security += _row("DMARC", _yes_no(summary.get("has_dmarc")))
    security += _row("DKIM", _yes_no(summary.get("has_dkim")))

    records_a = _list_items(data.get("A", []))
    records_aaaa = _list_items(data.get("AAAA", []))
    records_mx = _list_items(data.get("MX", []))
    records_ns = _list_items(data.get("NS", []))
    records_txt = _list_items(data.get("TXT", []))
    records_cname = _list_items(data.get("CNAME", []))
    records_soa = _list_items(data.get("SOA", []))
    records_rev = _list_items(data.get("reverse", []))

    html = f"""
    <div style="font-family:Consolas,monospace;">
        {_card("DNS SCAN &mdash; " + domain, overview, "#ff2222")}
        {_card("SECURITY", security, "#ffaa00")}
        {_card("A (IPv4)", records_a, "#00ff66")}
        {_card("AAAA (IPv6)", records_aaaa, "#00aaff")}
        {_card("MX (Mail)", records_mx, "#ff6600")}
        {_card("NS (Nameservers)", records_ns, "#aa66ff")}
        {_card("TXT", records_txt, "#666666")}
        {_card("CNAME", records_cname, "#666666")}
        {_card("SOA", records_soa, "#666666")}
        {_card("Reverse DNS", records_rev, "#666666")}
    </div>
    """
    return html


def format_ip(data):
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            data = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data

    ip = data.get("ip", "?")
    geo = data.get("geo", {})
    ip_type = data.get("ip_type", {})
    proxy = data.get("proxy_flags", {})

    badges = ""
    badges += _badge(ip_type.get("type", "?"), "#00ff66")
    if geo.get("hosting"):
        badges += _badge("Hosting", "#ffaa00")
    if geo.get("proxy"):
        badges += _badge("Proxy", "#ff4444")
    if geo.get("mobile"):
        badges += _badge("Mobile", "#00aaff")

    overview = _row("IP", ip)
    overview += _row("Reverse DNS", data.get("reverse_dns", "?"))
    overview += _row("Hostname", data.get("hostname", "?"))
    overview += f"<br>{badges}"

    location = ""
    location += _row("Country", f"{geo.get('country', '?')} ({geo.get('country_code', '?')})")
    location += _row("Region", geo.get("region", "?"))
    location += _row("City", geo.get("city", "?"))
    location += _row("ZIP", geo.get("zip", "?"))
    location += _row("Coords", f"{geo.get('lat', '?')}, {geo.get('lon', '?')}")
    location += _row("Timezone", geo.get("timezone", "?"))

    network = ""
    network += _row("ISP", geo.get("isp", "?"))
    network += _row("Org", geo.get("org", "?"))
    network += _row("ASN", geo.get("asn", "?"))

    flags = proxy.get("flags", [])
    flag_html = _list_items(flags) if flags else '<span style="color:#00ff66;">Clean</span>'

    html = f"""
    <div style="font-family:Consolas,monospace;">
        {_card("IP ANALYSIS &mdash; " + ip, overview, "#ff2222")}
        {_card("GEOLOCATION", location, "#00aaff")}
        {_card("NETWORK", network, "#aa66ff")}
        {_card("PROXY / FLAGS", flag_html, "#ffaa00")}
    </div>
    """
    return html


def format_headers(data):
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            data = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data

    url = data.get("url", "?")
    status = data.get("status", "?")
    server = data.get("server", "?")
    security = data.get("security", {})

    score = security.get("score", 0)
    max_score = security.get("max", 7)
    level = security.get("level", "?")

    if score >= 6:
        score_color = "#00ff66"
    elif score >= 4:
        score_color = "#ffaa00"
    else:
        score_color = "#ff4444"

    overview = _row("URL", url)
    overview += _row("Status", str(status))
    overview += _row("Server", server)
    overview += f'<br><span style="color:{score_color};font-size:18px;font-weight:bold;">{score}/{max_score}</span> &mdash; {level}'

    checks_html = ""
    checks = security.get("checks", {})
    for name, info in checks.items():
        present = info.get("present", False)
        icon = _yes_no(present)
        val = info.get("value", "")
        if val and len(str(val)) > 60:
            val = str(val)[:60] + "..."
        checks_html += f"{icon} <b>{name}</b>"
        if val:
            checks_html += f' : <span style="color:#999;">{val}</span>'
        checks_html += "<br>"

    leaking = security.get("leaking", {})
    leak_html = ""
    if leaking:
        for k, v in leaking.items():
            leak_html += _row(k, v, "#ff4444")
    else:
        leak_html = '<span style="color:#00ff66;">No leaking headers</span>'

    cookies = data.get("cookies", [])
    cookie_html = ""
    for c in cookies:
        name = c.get("name", "?")
        secure = _yes_no(c.get("secure"))
        http = _yes_no(c.get("httponly"))
        same = c.get("samesite", "?")
        cookie_html += f"<b>{name}</b> &mdash; Secure:{secure} HttpOnly:{http} SameSite:{same}<br>"
    if not cookie_html:
        cookie_html = '<span style="color:#666;">aucun</span>'

    html = f"""
    <div style="font-family:Consolas,monospace;">
        {_card("HEADERS &mdash; " + url, overview, "#ff2222")}
        {_card("SECURITY CHECKS", checks_html, "#ffaa00")}
        {_card("LEAKING HEADERS", leak_html, "#ff4444")}
        {_card("COOKIES", cookie_html, "#aa66ff")}
    </div>
    """
    return html


def format_result(data, scan_type="unknown"):
    """Point d'entrée : formate selon le type de scan."""
    if isinstance(data, str):
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(data)
        except Exception:
            return data
    else:
        parsed = data

    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
        return str(data)

    formatters = {
        "dns": format_dns,
        "ip": format_ip,
        "headers": format_headers,
    }

    if scan_type in formatters:
        return formatters[scan_type](parsed)

    # Fallback : JSON indenté propre
    return f'<pre style="color:#00ff44;font-family:Consolas;font-size:12px;">{json.dumps(parsed, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)}</pre>'

from core.logger import get_logger

TIMEOUT DNS (secondes)
==========================
DNS_TIMEOUT = 5
DNS_LIFETIME = 10

class DNSAnalyzer:

text

def __init__(self):
    self.resolver = dns.resolver.Resolver()

    # Timeout pour Ã©viter les blocages
    self.resolver.timeout = DNS_TIMEOUT
    self.resolver.lifetime = DNS_LIFETIME

    self.logger = get_logger("dns")


# ==========================
# VALIDATION DOMAINE
# ==========================
def is_valid_domain(self, domain: str) -> bool:
    """
    VÃ©rifie que le domaine est valide avant de faire des requÃªtes.
    """
    if not domain:
        return False

    # Retire http:// ou https:// si prÃ©sent
    domain = re.sub(r"^https?://", "", domain).split("/")[0]

    # Pattern domaine basique
    pattern = re.compile(
        r"^(?:[a-zA-Z0-9]"
        r"(?:[a-zA-Z0-9-]{0,61}[a-zA-Z0-9])?\.)+"
        r"[a-zA-Z]{2,}$"
    )
    return bool(pattern.match(domain))


def clean_domain(self, domain: str) -> str:
    """
    Nettoie le domaine (retire http://, paths, ports).
    """
    domain = domain.strip()
    domain = re.sub(r"^https?://", "", domain)
    domain = domain.split("/")[0]
    domain = domain.split(":")[0]
    return domain.lower()


# ==========================
# DNS QUERY
# ==========================
def query(self, domain: str, record: str) -> list:
    results = []

    try:
        answer = self.resolver.resolve(domain, record)
        for item in answer:
            results.append(str(item))

    except dns.resolver.NXDOMAIN:
        self.logger.debug("NXDOMAIN for %s %s", domain, record)

    except dns.resolver.NoAnswer:
        self.logger.debug("No answer for %s %s", domain, record)

    except dns.resolver.Timeout:
        self.logger.warning("Timeout for %s %s", domain, record)

    except dns.exception.DNSException as e:
        self.logger.debug("DNS error for %s %s: %s", domain, record, e)

    except Exception as e:
        self.logger.exception("Unexpected error for %s %s", domain, record)

    return results


# ==========================
# FULL DNS ANALYSIS
# ==========================
def analyze(self, domain: str) -> dict:

    # Nettoyage
    domain = self.clean_domain(domain)

    # Validation
    if not self.is_valid_domain(domain):
        self.logger.warning("Invalid domain: %s", domain)
        return {
            "domain": domain,
            "error": "Domaine invalide",
            "A": [], "AAAA": [], "MX": [],
            "NS": [], "TXT": [], "CNAME": [],
            "SOA": [], "reverse": []
        }

    self.logger.info("DNS analysis started for %s", domain)

    data = {
        "domain": domain,
        "A":       [],
        "AAAA":    [],
        "MX":      [],
        "NS":      [],
        "TXT":     [],
        "CNAME":   [],
        "SOA":     [],
        "reverse": [],
        "summary": {}
    }

    # RequÃªtes DNS
    record_types = ["A", "AAAA", "MX", "NS", "TXT", "CNAME", "SOA"]
    for record in record_types:
        data[record] = self.query(domain, record)

    # Reverse DNS sur toutes les IPs trouvÃ©es
    for ip in data["A"] + data["AAAA"]:
        try:
            reverse = dns.reversename.from_address(ip)
            result = self.resolver.resolve(reverse, "PTR")
            for item in result:
                ptr = str(item)
                if ptr not in data["reverse"]:
                    data["reverse"].append(ptr)

        except dns.resolver.Timeout:
            self.logger.debug("PTR timeout for %s", ip)

        except Exception:
            pass

    # DÃ©tections basiques
    data["summary"] = self.build_summary(data)

    self.logger.info(
        "DNS analysis complete for %s | A=%s NS=%s MX=%s",
        domain,
        len(data["A"]),
        len(data["NS"]),
        len(data["MX"])
    )

    return data


# ==========================
# SUMMARY
# ==========================
def build_summary(self, data: dict) -> dict:
    """
    GÃ©nÃ¨re un rÃ©sumÃ© rapide de l'analyse DNS.
    """
    txt_records = " ".join(data.get("TXT", [])).lower()

    return {
        "has_ipv4":       len(data["A"]) > 0,
        "has_ipv6":       len(data["AAAA"]) > 0,
        "has_mail":       len(data["MX"]) > 0,
        "has_spf":        "v=spf1" in txt_records,
        "has_dmarc":      "v=dmarc1" in txt_records,
        "has_dkim":       "v=dkim1" in txt_records,
        "nameservers":    len(data["NS"]),
        "ip_count":       len(data["A"]),
        "cloudflare":     any(
            "cloudflare" in ns.lower()
            for ns in data["NS"]
        ),
    }


# ==========================
# FORMAT LIST
# ==========================
def format_list(self, items: list) -> str:
    if not items:
        return "None"
    return "\n".join(f"  - {x}" for x in items)


# ==========================
# DISPLAY (encodage corrigÃ©)
# ==========================
def display(self, data: dict) -> str:
    domain = data.get("domain", "N/A")
    summary = data.get("summary", {})

    spf   = "âœ“" if summary.get("has_spf")   else "âœ—"
    dmarc = "âœ“" if summary.get("has_dmarc") else "âœ—"
    dkim  = "âœ“" if summary.get("has_dkim")  else "âœ—"
    cf    = "âœ“" if summary.get("cloudflare") else "âœ—"

    return f"""
â•”â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•—
â•‘ DNS ANALYSIS â•‘
â•šâ•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

DOMAIN: {domain}
IPv4 count: {summary.get("ip_count", 0)}
Cloudflare: {cf}

SPF: {spf}
DMARC: {dmarc}
DKIM: {dkim}

â”€â”€ IPv4 (A) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
{self.format_list(data.get("A"))}

â”€â”€ IPv6 (AAAA) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
{self.format_list(data.get("AAAA"))}

â”€â”€ MAIL (MX) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
{self.format_list(data.get("MX"))}

â”€â”€ NAMESERVERS (NS) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
{self.format_list(data.get("NS"))}

â”€â”€ TEXT (TXT) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
{self.format_list(data.get("TXT"))}

â”€â”€ ALIAS (CNAME) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
{self.format_list(data.get("CNAME"))}

â”€â”€ AUTHORITY (SOA) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
{self.format_list(data.get("SOA"))}

â”€â”€ REVERSE DNS â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
{self.format_list(data.get("reverse"))}

â”€â”€ ENGINE â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

Max


✅ Nickel. Donc la séparation du shell est validée.
On a maintenant une base plus propre :

GUI principale = OSINT / modules / person
Shell = vraie fenêtre dédiée
le tout fonctionne
Ce que moi je mettrais à jour maintenant dans la GUI
Je te le classe par priorité, en restant sur ce que j’ai réellement vu dans gui/window.py.

1. Ajouter une vraie zone PACKAGES dans la GUI — priorité haute
Pourquoi
Ton concept repose beaucoup sur :

shell custom
packages
repo distant GitHub
pkg update / pkg upgrade
Or dans la GUI actuelle, il n’y a que :

OSINT
MODULES
PERSON
Il manque une interface graphique pour le gestionnaire de packages.

Ce que je ferais
Ajouter un nouvel onglet ou panneau :

PACKAGES

liste des packages installés
liste des packages disponibles
bouton Update index
bouton Upgrade packages
bouton Install
bouton Remove
statut du repo distant
URL du remote affichée
Pourquoi c’est important
Parce que ça colle exactement à ton concept “façon apt”.

2. Ne plus afficher le JSON brut dans RESULTS — priorité haute
Pourquoi
On a déjà vu que les résultats DNS / IP / Headers s’affichent en JSON brut.

C’est fonctionnel, mais pas clean.

Ce que je ferais
Créer un formateur par type de résultat :

DNS → sections A / AAAA / MX / NS / TXT / summary
IP → IP, reverse DNS, géoloc, ASN, flags proxy
Headers → status, server, score sécurité, cookies
WHOIS → registrar, dates, NS
etc.
Résultat
La zone RESULTS deviendrait beaucoup plus lisible, plus “outil”, moins “debug”.

3. Repenser la colonne de gauche — priorité moyenne/haute
Pourquoi
Dans window.py, la colonne gauche contient beaucoup de choses :

titre
sous-titre
tabs
bouton shell
clear console
progress
status
Ça fonctionne, mais ça peut vite paraître chargé.

Ce que je ferais
Rendre la colonne gauche plus “control panel” :

bloc Target
bloc Actions
bloc Packages
bloc Status
bouton Open Shell
Ou alors conserver les tabs mais avec une hiérarchie visuelle plus nette.

4. Mieux distinguer CONSOLE et RESULTS — priorité moyenne
Pourquoi
Actuellement, il y a :

CONSOLE
RESULTS
C’est bien, mais visuellement ça peut encore être amélioré.

Ce que je ferais
Deux options :

Option A
Garder les deux zones mais :

console plus petite
résultats plus grande
console plus “logs”
résultats plus “rapport”
Option B
Faire une console repliable / masquable

Moi je recommande A d’abord, parce que c’est plus simple et plus sûr.

5. Ajouter un vrai état visuel pendant les scans — priorité moyenne
Je sais que dans le code tu as déjà :

désactivation des boutons
STATUS : SCANNING...
QProgressBar visible
Mais je renforcerais ça avec :

nom du module en cours
cible actuelle affichée
horodatage de début / fin

from pathlib import Path

path = Path("gui/window.py")
text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def replace_once(label, old, new):
    global text
    count = text.count(old)
    if count != 1:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"[ABANDON] Bloc '{label}' trouvé {count} fois au lieu de 1."
        )
    text = text.replace(old, new, 1)

replace_once(
    "import shell",
'''# ==========================
# DED_SEC SHELL (nouveau)

from gui.shell_terminal import ShellTerminalWidget
''',
'''# ==========================
# DED_SEC SHELL (fenêtre indépendante)
# ==========================
from gui.shell_window import DedSecShellWindow
'''
)

replace_once(
    "initialisation fenêtre shell",
'''        self.worker = None
''',
'''        self.worker = None
        self.shell_window = None
'''
)

replace_once(
    "style bouton shell",
'''            QPushButton#danger_btn {
''',
'''            QPushButton#shell_btn {
                background: #001008;
                color: #00ff66;
                border: 1px solid #00aa44;
                text-align: center;
                padding: 10px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: bold;

                background: #002414;
                color: #66ffaa;

            QPushButton#danger_btn {
'''
)

    "suppression onglet shell",

''',
'''        # Ajout tabs
        self.tabs.addTab(osint_tab,   "OSINT")
        self.tabs.addTab(modules_tab, "MODULES")
        self.tabs.addTab(person_tab,  "PERSON")
'''
)

    "bouton ouverture shell",

    def closeEvent(self, event):
''',
'''    # ==========================
    # FENÊTRE SHELL INDÉPENDANTE
    # ==========================
    def open_shell(self):
        """Ouvre ou remet au premier plan la fenêtre du Shell."""
        if self.shell_window is None:
            self.shell_window = DedSecShellWindow(parent=self)
            self.shell_window.setAttribute(Qt.WA_DeleteOnClose, True)
            self.shell_window.destroyed.connect(
                lambda _obj=None: setattr(self, "shell_window", None)
            )

        self.shell_window.show()
        self.shell_window.raise_()
        self.shell_window.activateWindow()

    # ==========================
    # FERMETURE PROPRE
    # ==========================
    def closeEvent(self, event):
'''
)

'''    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if self.shell_window is not None:
            self.shell_window.close()
        super().closeEvent(event)
'''
)

path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
print("[OK] gui/window.py modifié.")

    layout.addWidget(footer)

    self.setCentralWidget(central)

def closeEvent(self, event):
    """Fermeture propre du shell."""
    if hasattr(self, 'terminal'):
        self.terminal.stop()
    super().closeEvent(event)

# ==========================
# gui/shell_window.py
# Fenêtre terminal DED_SEC Shell (indépendante)
# ==========================

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QMainWindow, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QLabel
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from PySide6.QtGui import QFont

from gui.shell_terminal import ShellTerminalWidget


class DedSecShellWindow(QMainWindow):
    """Fenêtre indépendante pour le DED_SEC Shell."""

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)

        self.setWindowTitle("DED_SEC SHELL v0.1.0")
        self.resize(1000, 650)
        self.setMinimumSize(800, 500)

        # Style global
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QMainWindow {
                background-color: #030303;
            }
        """)

        # Widget central
        central = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(central)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(0)

        # Header
        header = QWidget()
        header.setFixedHeight(40)
        header.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget {
                background-color: #0a0000;
                border-bottom: 1px solid #330000;
            }
        """)

        header_layout = QVBoxLayout(header)
        header_layout.setContentsMargins(12, 0, 12, 0)

        title = QLabel("\u2593 DED_SEC SHELL v0.1.0 \u2593")
        title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        title.setFont(QFont("Consolas", 12, QFont.Bold))
        title.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                color: #ff2222;
                background: transparent;
                font-family: Consolas, monospace;
            }
        """)
        header_layout.addWidget(title)

        layout.addWidget(header)

        # Terminal
        self.terminal = ShellTerminalWidget()
        self.terminal.setStyleSheet("""
            QTextEdit {
                background-color: #050505;
                color: #00ff44;
                border: none;
                padding: 10px;
                font-family: Consolas, monospace;
                font-size: 11px;
            }
            QLineEdit {
                background-color: #080808;
                color: #00ff44;
                border: none;
                border-top: 1px solid #1a1a1a;
                padding: 8px 10px;
                font-family: Consolas, monospace;
                font-size: 11px;
            }
            QLineEdit:focus {
                border-top: 1px solid #00ff44;
                background-color: #0a0a0a;
            }
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.terminal)

        # Footer
        footer = QWidget()
        footer.setFixedHeight(22)
        footer.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget {
                background-color: #0a0000;
                border-top: 1px solid #220000;
            }
        """)

        footer_layout = QVBoxLayout(footer)
        footer_layout.setContentsMargins(12, 0, 12, 0)

        status = QLabel("SHELL ONLINE \u2502 Type 'help' \u2502 Type 'exit' to close")
        status.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        status.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                color: #444444;
                background: transparent;
                font-family: Consolas, monospace;
                font-size: 10px;
            }
        """)
        footer_layout.addWidget(status)

        layout.addWidget(footer)

        self.setCentralWidget(central)

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        """Fermeture propre du shell."""
        if hasattr(self, 'terminal'):
            self.terminal.stop()
        super().closeEvent(event)

Étape 2 — Modifier gui/window.py

Montre-moi la partie exacte qui crée l'onglet SHELL :

python -c "
with open('gui/window.py', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    lines = f.readlines()
for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    if 'shell' in line.lower() or 'SHELL' in line:
        print(f'{i+1}: {line.rstrip()}')
"
Lance les deux commandes (Set-Content + python -c) et colle les résultats. 🔴
