==========================
pkg/manager.py
Gestionnaire de packages DED_SEC (type apt)
==========================
import json
import os
import shutil
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path

from shell.prompt import Color

REMOTE_URL = "https://dedsecrootme.github.io/dedsec-repo"
TIMEOUT = 10

class PkgManager:
"""
Gestionnaire de packages du DED_SEC Shell.

text

Repo di> # ==========================
pkg/manager.py
Gestionnaire de packages DED_SEC (type apt)
==========================
import json
import os
import shutil
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path

from shell.prompt import Color

REMOTE_URL = "https://dedsecrootme.github.io/dedsec-repo"
TIMEOUT = 10

class PkgManager:
"""
Gestionnaire de packages du DED_SEC Shell.

text

Repo distant : GitHub Pages (priorité)
Repo local   : pkg/repo/ (fallback)
Installés    : <sandbox>/.config/packages/
Base données : <sandbox>/.config/packages.db.json
"""

def __init__(self, sandbox_root=None):
    # Repo local
    self.repo_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "repo"
    self.index_path = self.repo_dir / "index.json"

    # Config remote
    self.config = self._load_config()
    self.remote_url = self.config.get("remote", {}).get("url", REMOTE_URL)
    self.remote_enabled = self.config.get("remote", {}).get("enabled", True)

    # Dossier installation
    if sandbox_root is not None:
        self.base_dir = Path(sandbox_root)
    else:
        self.base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "dedsec_home"

    self.install_dir = self.base_dir / ".config" / "packages"
    self.db_path = self.base_dir / ".config" / "packages.db.json"
    self.install_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Cache de l index remote (chargé une fois par session)
    self._remote_index_cache = None

# ==========================
# CONFIG
# ==========================
def _load_config(self) -> dict:
    config_path = Path(__file__).parent / "config.json"
    if not config_path.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        with open(config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return {}

# ==========================
# BASE DE DONNÉES
# ==========================
def _load_db(self) -> dict:
    if not self.db_path.exists():
        return {"installed": {}}
    try:
        with open(self.db_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return {"installed": {}}

def _save_db(self, db: dict):
    with open(self.db_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(db, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

# ==========================
# RÉSEAU
# ==========================
def _fetch_url(self, url: str) -> str:
    """Télécharge une URL et retourne le contenu texte."""
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url,
            headers={"User-Agent": "DED_SEC-PKG/1.0"}
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=TIMEOUT) as resp:
            return resp.read().decode("utf-8")
    except urllib.error.URLError as e:
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None

def _fetch_json(self, url: str) -> dict:
    """Télécharge et parse un JSON depuis une URL."""
    content = self._fetch_url(url)
    if content is None:
        return None
    try:
        return json.loads(content)
    except Exception:
        return None

# ==========================
# INDEX
# ==========================
def _fetch_remote_index(self) -> list:
    """Télécharge l index depuis GitHub Pages."""
    if self._remote_index_cache is not None:
        return self._remote_index_cache

    url = f"{self.remote_url}/index.json"
    data = self._fetch_json(url)
    if data:
        self._remote_index_cache = data.get("packages", [])
        return self._remote_index_cache
    return []

def _load_local_index(self) -> list:
    """Charge l index local (fallback)."""
    if not self.index_path.exists():
        return []
    try:
        with open(self.index_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f).get("packages", [])
    except Exception:
        return []

def load_index(self) -> list:
    """Charge l index : remote en priorité, local en fallback."""
    if self.remote_enabled:
        remote = self._fetch_remote_index()
        if remote:
            return remote
    return self._load_local_index()

def get_repo_package(self, name: str) -> dict:
    for pkg in self.load_index():
        if pkg["name"].lower() == name.lower():
            return pkg
    return None

# ==========================
# PKG UPDATE
# ==========================
def update(self) -> int:
    """Télécharge l index depuis GitHub et compare avec installés."""
    print(f"\n  {Color.CYAN}[PKG] Contacting remote repo...{Color.RESET}")
    print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  {self.remote_url}{Color.RESET}\n")

    # Reset cache pour forcer un nouveau téléchargement
    self._remote_index_cache = None

    url = f"{self.remote_url}/index.json"
    data = self._fetch_json(url)

    if data is None:
        print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}[!] Remote inaccessible, fallback local{Color.RESET}")
        index = self._load_local_index()
        source = "local"
    else:
        index = data.get("packages", [])
        self._remote_index_cache = index
        # Sauvegarde l index local
        with open(self.index_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        source = "remote"

    if not index:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}[!] Index vide{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

    db = self._load_db()
    installed = db["installed"]

    print(f"  {Color.GREEN}[OK] Index chargé ({source}) — {len(index)} packages{Color.RESET}\n")

    # Affiche les mises à jour disponibles
    updates = []
    for pkg in index:
        name = pkg["name"]
        if name in installed:
            current = installed[name]["version"]
            latest = pkg["version"]
            if current != latest:
                updates.append((name, current, latest))

    if updates:
        print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}Mises à jour disponibles :{Color.RESET}")
        for name, current, latest in updates:
            print(
                f"  {Color.CYAN}  {name:<14}{Color.RESET} "
                f"v{current} {Color.GRAY}→{Color.RESET} "
                f"{Color.GREEN}v{latest}{Color.RESET}"
            )
        print(f"\n  {Color.YELLOW}Utilisez 'pkg upgrade' pour mettre à jour.{Color.RESET}")
    else:
        print(f"  {Color.GREEN}Tout est à jour.{Color.RESET}")

    print()
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG UPGRADE
# ==========================
def upgrade(self) -> int:
    """Met à jour tous les packages installés."""
    db = self._load_db()
    index = {p["name"]: p for p in self.load_index()}
    upgraded = 0
    failed = 0

    updates = []
    for name, info in db["installed"].items():
        pkg = index.get(name)
        if pkg and pkg["version"] != info["version"]:
            updates.append((name, info["version"], pkg["version"], pkg))

    if not updates:
        print(f"  {Color.GREEN}Tout est déjà à jour.{Color.RESET}")
        return 0

    print(f"\n  {Color.CYAN}[PKG] Upgrade — {len(updates)} package(s){Color.RESET}\n")

    for name, current, latest, pkg in updates:
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}Mise à jour : {name} "
            f"v{current} → v{latest}{Color.RESET}"
        )
        result = self._download_and_install(pkg)
        if result == 0:
            db["installed"][name]["version"] = latest
            db["installed"][name]["updated_at"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            upgraded += 1
            print(f"  {Color.GREEN}  ✓ {name} v{latest}{Color.RESET}")
        else:
            failed += 1
            print(f"  {Color.RED}  ✗ Échec : {name}{Color.RESET}")

    self._save_db(db)

    print(f"\n  {Color.GREEN}✓ {upgraded} mis à jour{Color.RESET}", end="")
    if failed:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ {failed} échec(s){Color.RESET}", end="")
    print("\n")
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG INSTALL
# ==========================
def install(self, name: str) -> int:
    pkg = self.get_repo_package(name)
    if pkg is None:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ Package introuvable : {name}{Color.RESET}")
        print(f"  {Color.CYAN}ℹ Tapez pkg search <mot> pour chercher.{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

    db = self._load_db()

    # Déjà installé ?
    if pkg["name"] in db["installed"]:
        installed = db["installed"][pkg["name"]]
        if installed["version"] == pkg["version"]:
            print(
                f"  {Color.YELLOW}⚠ {pkg['name']} v{pkg['version']} "
                f"est déjà installé.{Color.RESET}"
            )
            return 0

    # Dépendances
    deps = pkg.get("dependencies", [])
    if deps:
        print(f"  {Color.CYAN}ℹ Dépendances : {', '.join(deps)}{Color.RESET}")
        for dep in deps:
            if dep not in db["installed"]:
                print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}→ Installation de : {dep}{Color.RESET}")
                self.install(dep)
                db = self._load_db()

    print(
        f"\n  {Color.CYAN}↓ Installation de {pkg['name']} "
        f"v{pkg['version']}...{Color.RESET}"
    )

    result = self._download_and_install(pkg)
    if result != 0:
        return 1

    db["installed"][pkg["name"]] = {
        "version": pkg["version"],
        "description": pkg.get("description", ""),
        "category": pkg.get("category", "misc"),
        "author": pkg.get("author", "ded_sec"),
        "installed_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
    }
    self._save_db(db)

    print(f"  {Color.GREEN}✓ {pkg['name']} v{pkg['version']} installé{Color.RESET}\n")
    return 0

# ==========================
# TÉLÉCHARGEMENT RÉEL
# ==========================
def _download_and_install(self, pkg: dict) -> int:
    """
    Télécharge un package depuis GitHub Pages.
    Fallback sur le repo local si indisponible.
    """
    name = pkg["name"]
    dest_dir = self.install_dir / name

    # Nettoyage
    if dest_dir.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(dest_dir)
    dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Tentative remote
    if self.remote_enabled:
        success = self._download_from_remote(name, dest_dir)
        if success:
            return 0

    # Fallback local
    return self._install_from_local(name, dest_dir)

def _download_from_remote(self, name: str, dest_dir: Path) -> bool:
    """Télécharge manifest.json + main.py depuis GitHub Pages."""
    base = f"{self.remote_url}/{name}"
    files = ["manifest.json", "main.py"]
    downloaded = 0

    print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  → GitHub Pages...{Color.RESET}")

    for filename in files:
        url = f"{base}/{filename}"
        content = self._fetch_url(url)
        if content is not None:
            (dest_dir / filename).write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
            downloaded += 1
        else:
            print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}  ⚠ Impossible de télécharger : {filename}{Color.RESET}")

    if downloaded == len(files):
        print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  ✓ Téléchargé depuis GitHub{Color.RESET}")
        return True

    # Nettoyage si incomplet
    shutil.rmtree(dest_dir, ignore_errors=True)
    dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return False

def _install_from_local(self, name: str, dest_dir: Path) -> int:
    """Copie depuis le repo local."""
    src_dir = self.repo_dir / name
    if not src_dir.exists():
        print(f"  {Color.RED}  ✗ Package absent du repo local{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

    print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  → Repo local (fallback)...{Color.RESET}")
    try:
        shutil.copytree(src_dir, dest_dir, dirs_exist_ok=True)
        print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  ✓ Installé depuis local{Color.RESET}")
        return 0
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}  ✗ Erreur : {e}{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

# ==========================
# PKG REMOVE
# ==========================
def remove(self, name: str) -> int:
    db = self._load_db()
    if name not in db["installed"]:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ Package non installé : {name}{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

    dest_dir = self.install_dir / name
    try:
        if dest_dir.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(dest_dir)
        del db["installed"][name]
        self._save_db(db)
        print(f"  {Color.GREEN}✓ Supprimé : {name}{Color.RESET}")
        return 0
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ Erreur : {e}{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

# ==========================
# PKG LIST
# ==========================
def list_installed(self) -> int:
    db = self._load_db()
    installed = db["installed"]

    if not installed:
        print(f"  {Color.GRAY}(aucun package installé){Color.RESET}")
        return 0

    print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}Packages installés ({len(installed)}){Color.RESET}\n")
    for name, info in sorted(installed.items()):
        print(
            f"  {Color.GREEN}✓{Color.RESET} "
            f"{Color.WHITE}{name:<14}{Color.RESET} "
            f"v{info['version']:<8} "
            f"{Color.GRAY}{info.get('description', '')[:45]}{Color.RESET}"
        )
    print()
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG SEARCH
# ==========================
def search(self, query: str) -> int:
    index = self.load_index()
    if not index:
        print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}(repo vide){Color.RESET}")
        return 0

    query_lower = query.lower()
    matches = [
        p for p in index
        if query_lower in p["name"].lower()
        or query_lower in p.get("description", "").lower()
        or query_lower in p.get("category", "").lower()
    ]

    if not matches:
        print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}Aucun résultat pour : {query}{Color.RESET}")
        return 0

    print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}Résultats pour '{query}' ({len(matches)}){Color.RESET}\n")
    for pkg in matches:
        db = self._load_db()
        installed = pkg["name"] in db["installed"]
        status = f"{Color.GREEN}[installé]{Color.RESET}" if installed else ""
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}◆ {pkg['name']:<14}{Color.RESET} "
            f"v{pkg['version']:<8} "
            f"{Color.GRAY}{pkg.get('description', '')[:40]}{Color.RESET} "
            f"{status}"
        )
    print()
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG INFO
# ==========================
def info(self, name: str) -> int:
    pkg = self.get_repo_package(name)
    db = self._load_db()
    installed = db["installed"].get(name)

    if pkg is None and installed is None:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ Package inconnu : {name}{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

    print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}┌─ Package : {name} {'─' * 20}{Color.RESET}")
    if pkg:
        print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Version{Color.RESET}      : v{pkg.get('version', '?')}")
        print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Description{Color.RESET}  : {pkg.get('description', '?')}")
        print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Catégorie{Color.RESET}    : {pkg.get('category', 'misc')}")
        print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Auteur{Color.RESET}       : {pkg.get('author', 'ded_sec')}")
        deps = pkg.get("dependencies", [])
        print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Dépendances{Color.RESET}  : {', '.join(deps) if deps else 'aucune'}")
    if installed:
        print(f"  {Color.GREEN}Installé{Color.RESET}     : v{installed['version']} ({installed.get('installed_at', '?')})")
    else:
        print(f"  {Color.GRAY}Non installé{Color.RESET}")
    print(f"  {Color.BOLD}└{'─' * 32}{Color.RESET}\n")
    return 0
==========================
HELP
==========================
def pkg_help() -> str:
return f"""

{Color.GREEN}pkg list{Color.RESET} Liste les packages installés
{Color.GREEN}pkg search <mot>{Color.RESET} Cherche dans le repo
{Color.GREEN}pkg install <nom>{Color.RESET} Installe un package (+ deps)
{Color.GREEN}pkg remove <nom>{Color.RESET} Désinstalle un package
{Color.GREEN}pkg update{Color.RESET} Synchronise avec GitHub
{Color.GREEN}pkg upgrade{Color.RESET} Met à jour les packages
{Color.GREEN}pkg info <nom>{Color.RESET} Détails d'un package
"""

# ==========================
# pkg/manager.py
# Gestionnaire de packages DED_SEC (type apt)
# ==========================

import json
import os
import shutil
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path

from shell.prompt import Color

REMOTE_URL = "https://dedsecrootme.github.io/dedsec-repo"
TIMEOUT = 10


class PkgManager:
    """
    Gestionnaire de packages du DED_SEC Shell.

    Repo distant : GitHub Pages (priorité)
    Repo local   : pkg/repo/ (fallback)
    Installés    : <sandbox>/.config/packages/
    Base données : <sandbox>/.config/packages.db.json
    """

    def __init__(self, sandbox_root=None):
        # Repo local
        self.repo_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "repo"
        self.index_path = self.repo_dir / "index.json"

        # Config remote
        self.config = self._load_config()
        self.remote_url = self.config.get("remote", {}).get("url", REMOTE_URL)
        self.remote_enabled = self.config.get("remote", {}).get("enabled", True)

        # Dossier installation
        if sandbox_root is not None:
            self.base_dir = Path(sandbox_root)
        else:
            self.base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "dedsec_home"

        self.install_dir = self.base_dir / ".config" / "packages"
        self.db_path = self.base_dir / ".config" / "packages.db.json"
        self.install_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        # Cache de l index remote (chargé une fois par session)
        self._remote_index_cache = None

    # ==========================
    # CONFIG
    # ==========================
    def _load_config(self) -> dict:
        config_path = Path(__file__).parent / "config.json"
        if not config_path.exists():
            return {}
        try:
            with open(config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except Exception:
            return {}

    # ==========================
    # BASE DE DONNÉES
    # ==========================
    def _load_db(self) -> dict:
        if not self.db_path.exists():
            return {"installed": {}}
        try:
            with open(self.db_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except Exception:
            return {"installed": {}}

    def _save_db(self, db: dict):
        with open(self.db_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(db, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

    # ==========================
    # RÉSEAU
    # ==========================
    def _fetch_url(self, url: str) -> str:
        """Télécharge une URL et retourne le contenu texte."""
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                url,
                headers={"User-Agent": "DED_SEC-PKG/1.0"}
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=TIMEOUT) as resp:
                return resp.read().decode("utf-8")
        except urllib.error.URLError as e:
            return None
        except Exception:
            return None

    def _fetch_json(self, url: str) -> dict:
        """Télécharge et parse un JSON depuis une URL."""
        content = self._fetch_url(url)
        if content is None:
            return None
        try:
            return json.loads(content)
        except Exception:
            return None

    # ==========================
    # INDEX
    # ==========================
    def _fetch_remote_index(self) -> list:
        """Télécharge l index depuis GitHub Pages."""
        if self._remote_index_cache is not None:
            return self._remote_index_cache

        url = f"{self.remote_url}/index.json"
        data = self._fetch_json(url)
        if data:
            self._remote_index_cache = data.get("packages", [])
            return self._remote_index_cache
        return []

    def _load_local_index(self) -> list:
        """Charge l index local (fallback)."""
        if not self.index_path.exists():
            return []
        try:
            with open(self.index_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                return json.load(f).get("packages", [])
        except Exception:
            return []

    def load_index(self) -> list:
        """Charge l index : remote en priorité, local en fallback."""
        if self.remote_enabled:
            remote = self._fetch_remote_index()
            if remote:
                return remote
        return self._load_local_index()

    def get_repo_package(self, name: str) -> dict:
        for pkg in self.load_index():
            if pkg["name"].lower() == name.lower():
                return pkg
        return None

    # ==========================
    # PKG UPDATE
    # ==========================
    def update(self) -> int:
        """Télécharge l index depuis GitHub et compare avec installés."""
        print(f"\n  {Color.CYAN}[PKG] Contacting remote repo...{Color.RESET}")
        print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  {self.remote_url}{Color.RESET}\n")

        # Reset cache pour forcer un nouveau téléchargement
        self._remote_index_cache = None

        url = f"{self.remote_url}/index.json"
        data = self._fetch_json(url)

        if data is None:
            print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}[!] Remote inaccessible, fallback local{Color.RESET}")
            index = self._load_local_index()
            source = "local"
        else:
            index = data.get("packages", [])
            self._remote_index_cache = index
            # Sauvegarde l index local
            with open(self.index_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
            source = "remote"

        if not index:
            print(f"  {Color.RED}[!] Index vide{Color.RESET}")
            return 1

        db = self._load_db()
        installed = db["installed"]

        print(f"  {Color.GREEN}[OK] Index chargé ({source}) — {len(index)} packages{Color.RESET}\n")

        # Affiche les mises à jour disponibles
        updates = []
        for pkg in index:
            name = pkg["name"]
            if name in installed:
                current = installed[name]["version"]
                latest = pkg["version"]
                if current != latest:
                    updates.append((name, current, latest))

        if updates:
            print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}Mises à jour disponibles :{Color.RESET}")
            for name, current, latest in updates:
                print(
                    f"  {Color.CYAN}  {name:<14}{Color.RESET} "
                    f"v{current} {Color.GRAY}→{Color.RESET} "
                    f"{Color.GREEN}v{latest}{Color.RESET}"
                )
            print(f"\n  {Color.YELLOW}Utilisez 'pkg upgrade' pour mettre à jour.{Color.RESET}")
        else:
            print(f"  {Color.GREEN}Tout est à jour.{Color.RESET}")

        print()
        return 0

    # ==========================
    # PKG UPGRADE
    # ==========================
    def upgrade(self) -> int:
        """Met à jour tous les packages installés."""
        db = self._load_db()
        index = {p["name"]: p for p in self.load_index()}
        upgraded = 0
        failed = 0

        updates = []
        for name, info in db["installed"].items():
            pkg = index.get(name)
            if pkg and pkg["version"] != info["version"]:
                updates.append((name, info["version"], pkg["version"], pkg))

        if not updates:
            print(f"  {Color.GREEN}Tout est déjà à jour.{Color.RESET}")
            return 0

        print(f"\n  {Color.CYAN}[PKG] Upgrade — {len(updates)} package(s){Color.RESET}\n")

        for name, current, latest, pkg in updates:
            print(
                f"  {Color.CYAN}Mise à jour : {name} "
                f"v{current} → v{latest}{Color.RESET}"
            )
            result = self._download_and_install(pkg)
            if result == 0:
                db["installed"][name]["version"] = latest
                db["installed"][name]["updated_at"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                upgraded += 1
                print(f"  {Color.GREEN}  ✓ {name} v{latest}{Color.RESET}")
            else:
                failed += 1
                print(f"  {Color.RED}  ✗ Échec : {name}{Color.RESET}")

        self._save_db(db)

        print(f"\n  {Color.GREEN}✓ {upgraded} mis à jour{Color.RESET}", end="")
        if failed:
            print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ {failed} échec(s){Color.RESET}", end="")
        print("\n")
        return 0

    # ==========================
    # PKG INSTALL
    # ==========================
    def install(self, name: str) -> int:
        pkg = self.get_repo_package(name)
        if pkg is None:
            print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ Package introuvable : {name}{Color.RESET}")
            print(f"  {Color.CYAN}ℹ Tapez pkg search <mot> pour chercher.{Color.RESET}")
            return 1

        db = self._load_db()

        # Déjà installé ?
        if pkg["name"] in db["installed"]:
            installed = db["installed"][pkg["name"]]
            if installed["version"] == pkg["version"]:
                print(
                    f"  {Color.YELLOW}⚠ {pkg['name']} v{pkg['version']} "
                    f"est déjà installé.{Color.RESET}"
                )
                return 0

        # Dépendances
        deps = pkg.get("dependencies", [])
        if deps:
            print(f"  {Color.CYAN}ℹ Dépendances : {', '.join(deps)}{Color.RESET}")
            for dep in deps:
                if dep not in db["installed"]:
                    print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}→ Installation de : {dep}{Color.RESET}")
                    self.install(dep)
                    db = self._load_db()

        print(
            f"\n  {Color.CYAN}↓ Installation de {pkg['name']} "
            f"v{pkg['version']}...{Color.RESET}"
        )

        result = self._download_and_install(pkg)
        if result != 0:
            return 1

        db["installed"][pkg["name"]] = {
            "version": pkg["version"],
            "description": pkg.get("description", ""),
            "category": pkg.get("category", "misc"),
            "author": pkg.get("author", "ded_sec"),
            "installed_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        }
        self._save_db(db)

        print(f"  {Color.GREEN}✓ {pkg['name']} v{pkg['version']} installé{Color.RESET}\n")
        return 0

    # ==========================
    # TÉLÉCHARGEMENT RÉEL
    # ==========================
    def _download_and_install(self, pkg: dict) -> int:
        """
        Télécharge un package depuis GitHub Pages.
        Fallback sur le repo local si indisponible.
        """
        name = pkg["name"]
        dest_dir = self.install_dir / name

        # Nettoyage
        if dest_dir.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(dest_dir)
        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        # Tentative remote
        if self.remote_enabled:
            success = self._download_from_remote(name, dest_dir)
            if success:
                return 0

        # Fallback local
        return self._install_from_local(name, dest_dir)

    def _download_from_remote(self, name: str, dest_dir: Path) -> bool:
        """Télécharge manifest.json + main.py depuis GitHub Pages."""
        base = f"{self.remote_url}/{name}"
        files = ["manifest.json", "main.py"]
        downloaded = 0

        print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  → GitHub Pages...{Color.RESET}")

        for filename in files:
            url = f"{base}/{filename}"
            content = self._fetch_url(url)
            if content is not None:
                (dest_dir / filename).write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
                downloaded += 1
            else:
                print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}  ⚠ Impossible de télécharger : {filename}{Color.RESET}")

        if downloaded == len(files):
            print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  ✓ Téléchargé depuis GitHub{Color.RESET}")
            return True

        # Nettoyage si incomplet
        shutil.rmtree(dest_dir, ignore_errors=True)
        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return False

    def _install_from_local(self, name: str, dest_dir: Path) -> int:
        """Copie depuis le repo local."""
        src_dir = self.repo_dir / name
        if not src_dir.exists():
            print(f"  {Color.RED}  ✗ Package absent du repo local{Color.RESET}")
            return 1

        print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  → Repo local (fallback)...{Color.RESET}")
        try:
            shutil.copytree(src_dir, dest_dir, dirs_exist_ok=True)
            print(f"  {Color.GRAY}  ✓ Installé depuis local{Color.RESET}")
            return 0
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  {Color.RED}  ✗ Erreur : {e}{Color.RESET}")
            return 1

    # ==========================
    # PKG REMOVE
    # ==========================
    def remove(self, name: str) -> int:
        db = self._load_db()
        if name not in db["installed"]:
            print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ Package non installé : {name}{Color.RESET}")
            return 1

        dest_dir = self.install_dir / name
        try:
            if dest_dir.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(dest_dir)
            del db["installed"][name]
            self._save_db(db)
            print(f"  {Color.GREEN}✓ Supprimé : {name}{Color.RESET}")
            return 0
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ Erreur : {e}{Color.RESET}")
            return 1

    # ==========================
    # PKG LIST
    # ==========================
    def list_installed(self) -> int:
        db = self._load_db()
        installed = db["installed"]

        if not installed:
            print(f"  {Color.GRAY}(aucun package installé){Color.RESET}")
            return 0

        print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}Packages installés ({len(installed)}){Color.RESET}\n")
        for name, info in sorted(installed.items()):
            print(
                f"  {Color.GREEN}✓{Color.RESET} "
                f"{Color.WHITE}{name:<14}{Color.RESET} "
                f"v{info['version']:<8} "
                f"{Color.GRAY}{info.get('description', '')[:45]}{Color.RESET}"
            )
        print()
        return 0

    # ==========================
    # PKG SEARCH
    # ==========================
    def search(self, query: str) -> int:
        index = self.load_index()
        if not index:
            print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}(repo vide){Color.RESET}")
            return 0

        query_lower = query.lower()
        matches = [
            p for p in index
            if query_lower in p["name"].lower()
            or query_lower in p.get("description", "").lower()
            or query_lower in p.get("category", "").lower()
        ]

        if not matches:
            print(f"  {Color.YELLOW}Aucun résultat pour : {query}{Color.RESET}")
            return 0

        print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}Résultats pour '{query}' ({len(matches)}){Color.RESET}\n")
        for pkg in matches:
            db = self._load_db()
            installed = pkg["name"] in db["installed"]
            status = f"{Color.GREEN}[installé]{Color.RESET}" if installed else ""
            print(
                f"  {Color.CYAN}◆ {pkg['name']:<14}{Color.RESET} "
                f"v{pkg['version']:<8} "
                f"{Color.GRAY}{pkg.get('description', '')[:40]}{Color.RESET} "
                f"{status}"
            )
        print()
        return 0

    # ==========================
    # PKG INFO
    # ==========================
    def info(self, name: str) -> int:
        pkg = self.get_repo_package(name)
        db = self._load_db()
        installed = db["installed"].get(name)

        if pkg is None and installed is None:
            print(f"  {Color.RED}✗ Package inconnu : {name}{Color.RESET}")
            return 1

        print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}┌─ Package : {name} {'─' * 20}{Color.RESET}")
        if pkg:
            print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Version{Color.RESET}      : v{pkg.get('version', '?')}")
            print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Description{Color.RESET}  : {pkg.get('description', '?')}")
            print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Catégorie{Color.RESET}    : {pkg.get('category', 'misc')}")
            print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Auteur{Color.RESET}       : {pkg.get('author', 'ded_sec')}")
            deps = pkg.get("dependencies", [])
            print(f"  {Color.CYAN}Dépendances{Color.RESET}  : {', '.join(deps) if deps else 'aucune'}")
        if installed:
            print(f"  {Color.GREEN}Installé{Color.RESET}     : v{installed['version']} ({installed.get('installed_at', '?')})")
        else:
            print(f"  {Color.GRAY}Non installé{Color.RESET}")
        print(f"  {Color.BOLD}└{'─' * 32}{Color.RESET}\n")
        return 0


# ==========================
# HELP

    return f"""
  {Color.BOLD}PKG — Gestionnaire de packages{Color.RESET}

  {Color.GREEN}pkg list{Color.RESET}              Liste les packages installés
  {Color.GREEN}pkg search <mot>{Color.RESET}      Cherche dans le repo
  {Color.GREEN}pkg install <nom>{Color.RESET}     Installe un package (+ deps)
  {Color.GREEN}pkg remove <nom>{Color.RESET}      Désinstalle un package
  {Color.GREEN}pkg update{Color.RESET}            Synchronise avec GitHub
  {Color.GREEN}pkg upgrade{Color.RESET}           Met à jour les packages
  {Color.GREEN}pkg info <nom>{Color.RESET}        Détails d'un package
"""

Structure gh-repo/
text

gh-repo/
├── index.json          ← index des packages
├── osint/
│   ├── manifest.json   ← métadonnées
│   └── main.py         ← code du package
├── crypto/
├── ghostshell/
├── network/
└── person/
C'est exactement la bonne structure pour un repo GitHub Pages.

Problèmes confirmés
text

1. index.json         → encodage corrompu (réseau, fantôme)
2. manifest.json      → encodage corrompu (ghostshell)
3. main.py (osint)    → encodage corrompu + box-drawing corrompus
4. manager.py         → encodage corrompu + pas de vrai réseau
5. pkg/config.json    → n'existe pas
🎯 Plan d'action concret
Ce qu'on va faire dans l'ordre
text

1. Corriger tous les JSON (index + manifests)
2. Créer pkg/config.json avec l'URL GitHub
3. Réécrire manager.py avec vrai pkg update/upgrade
4. Tester pkg update → pkg upgrade dans le shell

(venv) PS C:\Users\Administrateur\DED_SEC-OSINT> cat pkg\manager.py

==========================
pkg/manager.py
Gestionnaire de packages DED_SEC (type apt)
==========================
import json
import os
import shutil
import time
import random
from pathlib import Path

from shell.prompt import Color

class PkgManager:
"""
Gestionnaire de packages du DED_SEC Shell.

text

Repo local : pkg/repo/ (index.json + dossiers de packages)
Packages installÃ©s : <sandbox>/.config/packages/
Base de donnÃ©es :   <sandbox>/.config/packages.db.json
"""

def __init__(self, sandbox_root=None):
    # DÃ©pÃ´t (source des packages)
    self.repo_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "repo"
    self.index_path = self.repo_dir / "index.json"

    # Dossier d'installation (dans le sandbox si dispo)
    if sandbox_root is not None:
        self.base_dir = Path(sandbox_root)
    else:
        self.base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "dedsec_home"

    self.install_dir = self.base_dir / ".config" / "packages"
    self.db_path = self.base_dir / ".config" / "packages.db.json"

    self.install_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# ==========================
# BASE DE DONNÃ‰ES
# ==========================
def _load_db(self) -> dict:
    """Charge la base des packages installÃ©s."""
    if not self.db_path.exists():
        return {"installed": {}}
    try:
        with open(self.db_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return {"installed": {}}

def _save_db(self, db: dict):
    with open(self.db_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(db, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

# ==========================
# REPO
# ==========================
def load_index(self) -> list:
    """Charge l'index du repo (liste des packages dispo)."""
    if not self.index_path.exists():
        return []
    try:
        with open(self.index_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f).get("packages", [])
    except Exception:
        return []

def get_repo_package(self, name: str) -> dict:
    for pkg in self.load_index():
        if pkg["name"].lower() == name.lower():
            return pkg
    return None

# ==========================
# PKG INSTALL
# ==========================
def install(self, name: str) -> int:
    pkg = self.get_repo_package(name)
    if pkg is None:
        print(
            f"  {Color.RED}âœ— Package introuvable : {name}{Color.RESET}"
        )
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}â„¹ Tapez 'pkg search <mot>' "
            f"pour chercher.{Color.RESET}"
        )
        return 1

    db = self._load_db()

    # DÃ©jÃ  installÃ© ?
    if pkg["name"] in db["installed"]:
        installed = db["installed"][pkg["name"]]
        if installed["version"] == pkg["version"]:
            print(
                f"  {Color.YELLOW}âš  {pkg['name']} v{pkg['version']} "
                f"est dÃ©jÃ  installÃ©.{Color.RESET}"
            )
            return 0

    # DÃ©pendances
    deps = pkg.get("dependencies", [])
    if deps:
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}â„¹ DÃ©pendances : {', '.join(deps)}"
            f"{Color.RESET}"
        )
        for dep in deps:
            if dep not in db["installed"]:
                print(
                    f"  {Color.YELLOW}â†’ Installation de la "
                    f"dÃ©pendance : {dep}{Color.RESET}"
                )
                self.install(dep)

    # "TÃ©lÃ©chargement" simulÃ© (repo local / futur VPS)
    src_dir = self.repo_dir / pkg["name"]
    dest_dir = self.install_dir / pkg["name"]

    print(
        f"  {Color.CYAN}â†“ TÃ©lÃ©chargement de {pkg['name']} "
        f"v{pkg['version']}...{Color.RESET}"
    )

    # Barre de progression simulÃ©e
    self._fake_progress()

    # Copie du package
    try:
        if dest_dir.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(dest_dir)
        if src_dir.exists():
            shutil.copytree(src_dir, dest_dir)
        else:
            dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}âœ— Erreur copie : {e}{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

    # Enregistre dans la DB
    db["installed"][pkg["name"]] = {
        "version": pkg["version"],
        "description": pkg.get("description", ""),
        "category": pkg.get("category", "misc"),
        "author": pkg.get("author", "ded_sec"),
        "installed_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
    }
    self._save_db(db)

    print(
        f"  {Color.GREEN}âœ“ Package installÃ© : "
        f"{pkg['name']} v{pkg['version']}{Color.RESET}"
    )
    print(
        f"  {Color.GRAY}  Emplacement : "
        f"{dest_dir}{Color.RESET}"
    )
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG REMOVE
# ==========================
def remove(self, name: str) -> int:
    db = self._load_db()

    if name not in db["installed"]:
        print(
            f"  {Color.RED}âœ— Package non installÃ© : {name}"
            f"{Color.RESET}"
        )
        return 1

    dest_dir = self.install_dir / name
    try:
        if dest_dir.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(dest_dir)
        del db["installed"][name]
        self._save_db(db)
        print(
            f"  {Color.GREEN}âœ“ Package supprimÃ© : {name}"
            f"{Color.RESET}"
        )
        return 0
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  {Color.RED}âœ— Erreur suppression : {e}{Color.RESET}")
        return 1

# ==========================
# PKG LIST
# ==========================
def list_installed(self) -> int:
    db = self._load_db()
    installed = db["installed"]

    if not installed:
        print("  (aucun package installÃ©)")
        return 0

    print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}Packages installÃ©s "
          f"({len(installed)}){Color.RESET}\n")
    for name, info in sorted(installed.items()):
        print(
            f"  {Color.GREEN}âœ“{Color.RESET} "
            f"{Color.WHITE}{name:<14}{Color.RESET} "
            f"v{info['version']:<8} {Color.GRAY}"
            f"{info.get('description', '')[:40]}{Color.RESET}"
        )
    print()
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG SEARCH
# ==========================
def search(self, query: str) -> int:
    index = self.load_index()
    if not index:
        print("  (repo vide)")
        return 0

    query_lower = query.lower()
    matches = [
        p for p in index
        if query_lower in p["name"].lower()
        or query_lower in p.get("description", "").lower()
        or query_lower in p.get("category", "").lower()
    ]

    if not matches:
        print(
            f"  {Color.YELLOW}â„¹ Aucun package trouvÃ© pour : {query}"
            f"{Color.RESET}"
        )
        return 0

    print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}RÃ©sultats pour '{query}' "
          f"({len(matches)}){Color.RESET}\n")
    for pkg in matches:
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}â—† {pkg['name']:<14}{Color.RESET} "
            f"v{pkg['version']:<8} {Color.GRAY}"
            f"{pkg.get('description', '')[:45]}{Color.RESET}"
        )
    print()
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG UPDATE
# ==========================
def update(self) -> int:
    print(
        f"  {Color.CYAN}â†» Mise Ã  jour de l'index du repo..."
        f"{Color.RESET}"
    )
    self._fake_progress()

    index = self.load_index()
    db = self._load_db()

    updates = 0
    for pkg in index:
        name = pkg["name"]
        if name in db["installed"]:
            current = db["installed"][name]["version"]
            if current != pkg["version"]:
                updates += 1

    print(
        f"  {Color.GREEN}âœ“ Index Ã  jour : {len(index)} packages "
        f"disponibles, {updates} mise(s) Ã  jour{Color.RESET}"
    )
    if updates:
        print(
            f"  {Color.YELLOW}â†’ Utilisez 'pkg upgrade' pour "
            f"mettre Ã  jour{Color.RESET}"
        )
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG UPGRADE
# ==========================
def upgrade(self) -> int:
    db = self._load_db()
    index = {p["name"]: p for p in self.load_index()}
    upgraded = 0

    for name, info in db["installed"].items():
        pkg = index.get(name)
        if pkg and pkg["version"] != info["version"]:
            print(
                f"  {Color.CYAN}â†‘ Mise Ã  jour de {name} "
                f"v{info['version']} â†’ v{pkg['version']}"
                f"{Color.RESET}"
            )
            self._fake_progress()
            db["installed"][name]["version"] = pkg["version"]
            upgraded += 1

    self._save_db(db)

    if upgraded:
        print(
            f"  {Color.GREEN}âœ“ {upgraded} package(s) mis Ã  jour"
            f"{Color.RESET}"
        )
    else:
        print(
            f"  {Color.GREEN}âœ“ Tout est dÃ©jÃ  Ã  jour{Color.RESET}"
        )
    return 0

# ==========================
# PKG INFO
# ==========================
def info(self, name: str) -> int:
    pkg = self.get_repo_package(name)
    db = self._load_db()
    installed = db["installed"].get(name)

    if pkg is None and installed is None:
        print(
            f"  {Color.RED}âœ— Package inconnu : {name}"
            f"{Color.RESET}"
        )
        return 1

    print(f"\n  {Color.BOLD}â”Œâ”€ Package : {name} "
          f"{'â”€' * 20}{Color.RESET}")
    if pkg:
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}Version{Color.RESET}     : "
            f"v{pkg.get('version', '?')}"
        )
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}Description{Color.RESET} : "
            f"{pkg.get('description', '?')}"
        )
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}CatÃ©gorie{Color.RESET}  : "
            f"{pkg.get('category', 'misc')}"
        )
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}Author{Color.RESET}     : "
            f"{pkg.get('author', 'ded_sec')}"
        )
        deps = pkg.get("dependencies", [])
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}DÃ©pendances{Color.RESET} : "
            f"{', '.join(deps) if deps else 'aucune'}"
        )
    if installed:
        print(
            f"  {Color.CYAN}InstallÃ©{Color.RESET}    : "
            f"v{installed['version']} "
            f"({installed.get('installed_at', '?')})"
        )
    print(f"  {Color.BOLD}â””{'â”€' * 30}{Color.RESET}\n")
    return 0

# ==========================
# PROGRESSION SIMULÃ‰E
# ==========================
def _fake_progress(self, steps: int = 12):
    """Petite barre de progression stylÃ©e (simulation rÃ©seau)."""
    print(f"  {Color.GRAY}[", end="", flush=True)
    for i in range(steps):
        time.sleep(0.04)
        print(f"{Color.RED}â–ˆ{Color.RESET}", end="", flush=True)
    print(f"{Color.GRAY}]{Color.RESET}")
==========================
HELP
==========================
def pkg_help() -> str:
return f"""
{Color.BOLD}â•”â• PKG â€” Gestionnaire de packages â•â•—{Color.RESET}

{Color.GREEN}pkg list{Color.RESET} Liste les packages installÃ©s
{Color.GREEN}pkg search <mot>{Color.RESET} Cherche dans le repo
{Color.GREEN}pkg install <nom>{Color.RESET} Installe un package (+ deps)
{Color.GREEN}pkg remove <nom>{Color.RESET} DÃ©sinstalle un package
{Color.GREEN}pkg update{Color.RESET} Met Ã  jour l'index du repo
{Color.GREEN}pkg upgrade{Color.RESET} Met Ã  jour les packages
{Color.GREEN}pkg info <nom>{Color.RESET} DÃ©tails d'un package
"""

Max


⚠️ Je vois ce qui s'est passé.
Le problème
Tu as testé mkdir, touch, rm dans PowerShell — pas dans le DED_SEC Shell.

text

# PowerShell a créé un vrai dossier "test" dans le projet
mkdir test  → a créé C:\Users\Administrateur\DED_SEC-OSINT\test\

# touch n'existe pas dans PowerShell → erreur normale
# rm a supprimé le vrai dossier test
Ce qu'il faut faire
Les commandes ls, mkdir, touch, rm sont des commandes du DED_SEC Shell, pas de PowerShell.

PowerShell

python dedsec_shell.py
Puis dans le shell DED_SEC :

text

mkdir test
touch test/fichier.txt
ls
rm test
Lance python dedsec_shell.py d'abord, puis tape les commandes dedans. 🔴




(venv) PS C:\Users\Administrateur\DED_SEC-OSINT> Set-Content -Path "shell\commands\filesystem.py" -Encoding UTF8 -Value @'

Commandes filesystem : ls, cd, pwd, cat, mkdir, rm, touch, echo

from shell.commands.base i> # ==========================

shell/commands/filesystem.py
Commandes filesystem : ls, cd, pwd, cat, mkdir, rm, touch, echo

from shell.commands.base import Command
from shell.prompt import Color

class LsCommand(Command):
name = "ls"
description = "Liste le contenu d'un dossier"
usage = "ls [path]"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    path = args[0] if args else None
    items = self.sandbox.list_dir(path)

    if items is None or not items:
        if path and not self.sandbox.exists(path):
            self.prompt.print_error(f"Dossier introuvable : {path}")
            return 1
        print("  (vide)")
        return 0

    for item in items:
        if item["is_dir"]:
            print(f"  {Color.BLUE}{item['name']}/{Color.RESET}")
        else:
            size = item["size"]
            if size < 1024:
                size_str = f"{size}B"
            elif size < 1024 * 1024:
                size_str = f"{size / 1024:.1f}KB"
            else:
                size_str = f"{size / (1024*1024):.1f}MB"

            print(
                f"  {Color.WHITE}{item['name']}{Color.RESET} "
                f"{Color.GRAY}({size_str}){Color.RESET}"
            )
    return 0
class CdCommand(Command):
name = "cd"
description = "Change de dossier"
usage = "cd <path>"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    if not args:
        self.sandbox.change_dir("~")
        return 0

    path = args[0]
    if self.sandbox.change_dir(path):
        return 0
    else:
        self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible d'aller à : {path}")
        return 1
class PwdCommand(Command):
name = "pwd"
description = "Affiche le dossier courant"
usage = "pwd"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    print(f"  {self.sandbox.get_cwd_display()}")
    return 0
class CatCommand(Command):
name = "cat"
description = "Affiche le contenu d'un fichier"
usage = "cat <file>"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    if not args:
        self.prompt.print_error("Usage: cat <file>")
        return 1

    content = self.sandbox.read_file(args[0])
    if content is None:
        self.prompt.print_error(f"Fichier introuvable : {args[0]}")
        return 1

    print(content)
    return 0
class MkdirCommand(Command):
name = "mkdir"
description = "Crée un nouveau dossier"
usage = "mkdir <dirname>"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    if not args:
        self.prompt.print_error("Usage: mkdir <dirname>")
        return 1

    if self.sandbox.make_dir(args[0]):
        self.prompt.print_success(f"Dossier créé : {args[0]}")
        return 0
    else:
        self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible de créer : {args[0]}")
        return 1
class RmCommand(Command):
name = "rm"
description = "Supprime un fichier ou dossier"
usage = "rm <path>"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    if not args:
        self.prompt.print_error("Usage: rm <path>")
        return 1

    path = args[0]

    if not self.sandbox.exists(path):
        self.prompt.print_error(f"N'existe pas : {path}")
        return 1

    try:
        confirm = input(
            f"{Color.YELLOW}\u26a0 Supprimer '{path}' ? (y/n) : {Color.RESET}"
        ).strip().lower()
    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
        print()
        return 1

    if confirm not in ("y", "yes", "o", "oui"):
        self.prompt.print_info("Annulé.")
        return 0

    if self.sandbox.remove(path):
        self.prompt.print_success(f"Supprimé : {path}")
        return 0
    else:
        self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible de supprimer : {path}")
        return 1
class TouchCommand(Command):
name = "touch"
description = "Crée un fichier vide"
usage = "touch <filename>"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    if not args:
        self.prompt.print_error("Usage: touch <filename>")
        return 1

    if self.sandbox.write_file(args[0], ""):
        self.prompt.print_success(f"Fichier créé : {args[0]}")
        return 0
    else:
        self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible de créer : {args[0]}")
        return 1
class EchoCommand(Command):
name = "echo"
description = "Affiche du texte ou écrit dans un fichier"
usage = "echo <text> [> file]"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    if not args:
        print()
        return 0

    if ">" in args:
        idx = args.index(">")
        text = " ".join(args[:idx])
        if idx + 1 >= len(args):
            self.prompt.print_error("Nom de fichier manquant après >")
            return 1
        filename = args[idx + 1]

        if self.sandbox.write_file(filename, text):
            self.prompt.print_success(f"Écrit dans : {filename}")
            return 0
        else:
            self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible d'écrire : {filename}")
            return 1

    print(" ".join(args))
    return 0

# ==========================
# shell/commands/filesystem.py
# Commandes filesystem : ls, cd, pwd, cat, mkdir, rm, touch, echo
# ==========================

from shell.commands.base import Command
from shell.prompt import Color


class LsCommand(Command):
    name = "ls"
    description = "Liste le contenu d'un dossier"
    usage = "ls [path]"
    category = "filesystem"

    def execute(self, args):
        path = args[0] if args else None
        items = self.sandbox.list_dir(path)

        if items is None or not items:
            if path and not self.sandbox.exists(path):
                self.prompt.print_error(f"Dossier introuvable : {path}")
                return 1
            print("  (vide)")
            return 0

        for item in items:
            if item["is_dir"]:
                print(f"  {Color.BLUE}{item['name']}/{Color.RESET}")
            else:
                size = item["size"]
                if size < 1024:
                    size_str = f"{size}B"
                elif size < 1024 * 1024:
                    size_str = f"{size / 1024:.1f}KB"
                else:
                    size_str = f"{size / (1024*1024):.1f}MB"

                print(
                    f"  {Color.WHITE}{item['name']}{Color.RESET} "
                    f"{Color.GRAY}({size_str}){Color.RESET}"
                )
        return 0


class CdCommand(Command):
    name = "cd"
    description = "Change de dossier"
    usage = "cd <path>"
    category = "filesystem"

    def execute(self, args):
        if not args:
            self.sandbox.change_dir("~")
            return 0

        path = args[0]
        if self.sandbox.change_dir(path):
            return 0
        else:
            self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible d'aller à : {path}")
            return 1


class PwdCommand(Command):
    name = "pwd"
    description = "Affiche le dossier courant"
    usage = "pwd"
    category = "filesystem"

    def execute(self, args):
        print(f"  {self.sandbox.get_cwd_display()}")
        return 0


class CatCommand(Command):
    name = "cat"
    description = "Affiche le contenu d'un fichier"
    usage = "cat <file>"
    category = "filesystem"

    def execute(self, args):
        if not args:
            self.prompt.print_error("Usage: cat <file>")
            return 1

        content = self.sandbox.read_file(args[0])
        if content is None:
            self.prompt.print_error(f"Fichier introuvable : {args[0]}")
            return 1

        print(content)
        return 0

    name = "rm"

    category = "filesystem"

    def execute(self, args):
        if not args:
            self.prompt.print_error("Usage: rm <path>")
            return 1

        path = args[0]

        if not self.sandbox.exists(path):
            self.prompt.print_error(f"N'existe pas : {path}")
            return 1

        try:
            confirm = input(
                f"{Color.YELLOW}\u26a0 Supprimer '{path}' ? (y/n) : {Color.RESET}"
            ).strip().lower()
        except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
            print()
            return 1

        if confirm not in ("y", "yes", "o", "oui"):
            self.prompt.print_info("Annulé.")
            return 0

        if self.sandbox.remove(path):
            self.prompt.print_success(f"Supprimé : {path}")
            return 0
        else:
            self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible de supprimer : {path}")
            return 1

        if not args:

            return 1


class EchoCommand(Command):
    name = "echo"
    description = "Affiche du texte ou écrit dans un fichier"
    usage = "echo <text> [> file]"
    category = "filesystem"

    def execute(self, args):
        if not args:
            print()
            return 0

        if ">" in args:
            idx = args.index(">")
            text = " ".join(args[:idx])
            if idx + 1 >= len(args):
                self.prompt.print_error("Nom de fichier manquant après >")
                return 1
            filename = args[idx + 1]

            if self.sandbox.write_file(filename, text):
                self.prompt.print_success(f"Écrit dans : {filename}")
                return 0
            else:
                self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible d'écrire : {filename}")
                return 1

        print(" ".join(args))
        return 0

from shell.prompt import Color

class LsCommand(Command):

description = "Liste le contenu d'un dossier"
usage = "ls [path]"
category = "filesystem"

text

def execute(self, args):
    path = args[0] if args else None
    items = self.sandbox.list_dir(path)

    if items is None or not items:
        if path and not self.sandbox.exists(path):
            self.prompt.print_error(f"Dossier introuvable : {path}")
            return 1
        print("  (vide)")
        return 0

    for item in items:
        if item["is_dir"]:
            print(f"  {Color.BLUE}{item['name']}/{Color.RESET}")
        else:
            size = item["size"]
            if size < 1024:
                size_str = f"{size}B"
            elif size < 1024 * 1024:
                size_str = f"{size / 1024:.1f}KB"
            else:
                size_str = f"{size / (1024*1024):.1f}MB"

            print(
                f"  {Color.WHITE}{item['name']}{Color.RESET} "
                f"{Color.GRAY}({size_str}){Color.RESET}"
            )
    return 0

    if not args:
        self.prompt.print_error("Usage: rm <path>")
        return 1

    path = args[0]

    if not self.sandbox.exists(path):
        self.prompt.print_error(f"N'existe pas : {path}")
        return 1

    # Confirmation
    try:
        confirm = input(
            f"{Color.YELLOW}âš  Supprimer '{path}' ? (y/n) : {Color.RESET}"
        ).strip().lower()
    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
        print()
        return 1

    if confirm not in ("y", "yes", "o", "oui"):
        self.prompt.print_info("AnnulÃ©.")
        return 0

    if self.sandbox.remove(path):
        self.prompt.print_success(f"SupprimÃ© : {path}")
        return 0
    else:
        self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible de supprimer : {path}")
        return 1

            self.prompt.print_error("Nom de fichier manquant aprÃ¨s >")

            return 0
        else:
            self.prompt.print_error(f"Impossible d'Ã©crire : {filename}")

    return 0

    return 0

# ==========================
# shell/core.py
# Moteur du shell DED_SEC
# Parse, exécute, boucle principale
# ==========================

import sys
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path

from shell.prompt import Prompt, Color
from shell.sandbox import Sandbox

class DedSecShell:
    """Moteur principal du shell DED_SEC"""

    def __init__(self, sandbox_root: str = None):
        self.start_time = time.time()
        self.running = True
        self.history = []

        # Initialisation sandbox + prompt
        self.sandbox = Sandbox(root_name=sandbox_root or "dedsec_home")
        self.prompt = Prompt(self.sandbox)

        # Dictionnaire des commandes
        self.commands = {}
        self._load_commands()

    def _load_commands(self):
        """Charge toutes les commandes disponibles."""
        # Filesystem
        try:
            from shell.commands.filesystem import (
                LsCommand, CdCommand, PwdCommand,
                CatCommand, MkdirCommand, RmCommand,
                TouchCommand, EchoCommand
            )
            self._register(LsCommand)
            self._register(CdCommand)
            self._register(PwdCommand)
            self._register(CatCommand)
            self._register(MkdirCommand)
            self._register(RmCommand)
            self._register(TouchCommand)
            self._register(EchoCommand)
        except ImportError as e:
            print(f"[!] Erreur chargement filesystem: {e}")

        # System
        try:
            from shell.commands.system import (
                WhoamiCommand, HelpCommand, ClearCommand,
                ExitCommand, HistoryCommand, UptimeCommand
            )
            self._register(WhoamiCommand)
            self._register(HelpCommand)
            self._register(ClearCommand)
            self._register(ExitCommand)
            self._register(HistoryCommand)
            self._register(UptimeCommand)
        except ImportError as e:
            print(f"[!] Erreur chargement system: {e}")

        # Info
        try:
            from shell.commands.info import (
                AboutCommand, VersionCommand, SysinfoCommand
            )
            self._register(AboutCommand)
            self._register(VersionCommand)
            self._register(SysinfoCommand)
        except ImportError as e:
            print(f"[!] Erreur chargement info: {e}")

        # Packages
        try:
            from shell.commands.packages import PkgCommand
            self._register(PkgCommand)
        except ImportError as e:
            print(f"[!] Erreur chargement packages: {e}")

    def _register(self, cmd_class):
        """Enregistre une commande dans le dictionnaire."""
        cmd = cmd_class(self)
        self.commands[cmd.name] = cmd

    def parse(self, line: str):
        """Parse une ligne de commande."""
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            return None, []

        parts = []
        current = ""
        in_quote = False
        quote_char = ""

        for c in line:
            if in_quote:
                if c == quote_char:
                    in_quote = False
                else:
                    current += c
            elif c in ('"', "'"):
                in_quote = True
                quote_char = c
            elif c == " ":
                if current:
                    parts.append(current)
                    current = ""
            else:
                current += c

        if current:
            parts.append(current)

        if not parts:
            return None, []

        return parts[0], parts[1:]

    def execute(self, line: str) -> int:
        """Parse et exécute une commande."""
        if not line or not line.strip():
            return 0

        # Skip commentaires
        if line.strip().startswith("#"):
            return 0

        cmd_name, args = self.parse(line)

        if cmd_name is None:
            return 0

        # Historique
        self.history.append(line.strip())

        if cmd_name in self.commands:
            try:
                return self.commands[cmd_name].execute(args)
            except Exception as e:
                self.prompt.print_error(f"Erreur: {e}")
                return 1
        else:
            self.prompt.print_error(f"Commande inconnue: {cmd_name}")
            self.prompt.print_info(f"Tapez 'help' pour voir les commandes disponibles")
            return 1

    def run(self):
        """Boucle principale du shell."""
        self.prompt.print_banner()

        while self.running:
            try:
                line = input(self.prompt.render())
            except EOFError:
                print()
                break
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                print(f"\n  {Color.YELLOW}^C{Color.RESET}")
                continue

            if not line or not line.strip():
                continue

            result = self.execute(line)

            if result == 99:
                self.running = False
                break

    def run_command(self, line: str):
        """Exécute une commande unique (mode one-shot)."""
        result = self.execute(line)
        if result == 99:
            self.running = False
        return result

    def stop(self):
        """Arrête le shell proprement."""
        self.running = False

2. shell/sandbox.py

Set-Content -Path "shell\sandbox.py" -Encoding UTF8 -Value @'
# ==========================
# shell/sandbox.py
# Système de fichiers virtuel
# ==========================

import os
from pathlib import Path


class Sandbox:
    """
    Gère le filesystem virtuel du shell.
    L'utilisateur ne peut pas sortir de son dossier home.
    """

    def __init__(self, root_name: str = "dedsec_home"):
        base = Path(__file__).parent.parent
        self.root = base / root_name
        self.root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self._init_default_dirs()
        self.cwd = self.root

    def _init_default_dirs(self):
        """Crée les dossiers par défaut."""
        defaults = ["Documents", "Downloads", "Scripts", "Reports", ".config"]
        for d in defaults:
            (self.root / d).mkdir(exist_ok=True)

        welcome = self.root / "README.txt"
        if not welcome.exists():
            welcome.write_text(
                "Welcome to DED_SEC Shell!\n"
                "Type 'help' to see available commands.\n",
                encoding="utf-8"
            )

    def get_cwd(self) -> Path:
        """Retourne le dossier courant."""
        return self.cwd

    def get_cwd_display(self) -> str:
        """Retourne le chemin courant à afficher (style Linux)."""
        try:
            rel = self.cwd.relative_to(self.root)
            if str(rel) == ".":
                return "~"
            return "~/" + str(rel).replace("\\", "/")
        except Exception:
            return "~"

    def resolve_path(self, path: str) -> Path:
        """Résout un chemin donné par l'utilisateur. Empêche de sortir du sandbox."""
        path = path.strip()

        if path == "~" or path == "":
            return self.root

        if path.startswith("/"):
            target = self.root / path.lstrip("/")
        elif path.startswith("~/"):
            target = self.root / path[2:]
        else:
            target = self.cwd / path

        try:
            target = target.resolve()
        except Exception:
            return self.cwd

        try:
            target.relative_to(self.root.resolve())
        except ValueError:
            return self.root

        return target

    def change_dir(self, path: str) -> bool:
        """Change de dossier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)
        if not target.exists():
            return False
        if not target.is_dir():
            return False
        self.cwd = target
        return True

    def list_dir(self, path: str = None) -> list:
        """Liste le contenu d'un dossier."""
        if path is None:
            target = self.cwd
        else:
            target = self.resolve_path(path)

        if not target.exists() or not target.is_dir():
            return []

        items = []
        for item in sorted(target.iterdir()):
            items.append({
                "name": item.name,
                "is_dir": item.is_dir(),
                "size": item.stat().st_size if item.is_file() else 0,
            })
        return items

    def read_file(self, path: str) -> str:
        """Lit le contenu d'un fichier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)
        if not target.exists() or not target.is_file():
            return None
        try:
            return target.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] {e}"

    def write_file(self, path: str, content: str) -> bool:
        """Écrit dans un fichier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)
        try:
            target.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    def make_dir(self, path: str) -> bool:
        """Crée un dossier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)
        try:
            target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    def remove(self, path: str) -> bool:
        """Supprime un fichier ou dossier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)
        if target == self.root:
            return False
        try:
            if target.is_file():
                target.unlink()
            elif target.is_dir():
                import shutil
                shutil.rmtree(target)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    def exists(self, path: str) -> bool:
        """Vérifie si un chemin existe."""
        return self.resolve_path(path).exists()

# ==========================
# shell/commands/base.py
# Classe de base pour toutes les commandes
# ==========================


class Command:
    """
    Classe de base pour toutes les commandes du shell.
    Chaque commande doit hériter de cette classe.
    """

    name = "unknown"
    description = "No description"
    usage = ""
    category = "misc"

    def __init__(self, shell):
        """
        shell = référence au shell principal
        (donne accès à sandbox, prompt, etc.)
        """
        self.shell = shell
        self.sandbox = shell.sandbox
        self.prompt = shell.prompt

    def execute(self, args: list) -> int:
        """
        Exécute la commande.
        Retourne un code de sortie :
        0  = succès
        1  = erreur
        99 = commande spéciale (ex: exit)
        """
        raise NotImplementedError(
            f"Command {self.name} doit implémenter execute()"
        )

    def help(self) -> str:
        """Retourne l aide de la commande."""
        return (
            f"{self.name} - {self.description}\n"
            f"Usage: {self.usage}"
        )

    def execute(self, line: str) -> int:

            return 0

        while self.running:

                line = input(self.prompt.render())

                print()
                break

        result = self.execute(line)
        if result == 99:
            self.running = False
        return result

    def stop(self):
        """Arrête le shell proprement."""
        self.running = False
2. shell/sandbox.py
Python

# ==========================
# shell/sandbox.py
# Système de fichiers virtuel
# ==========================

import os
from pathlib import Path


class Sandbox:
    """
    Gère le filesystem virtuel du shell.
    L'utilisateur ne peut pas sortir de son dossier home.
    """

    def __init__(self, root_name: str = "dedsec_home"):
        # Dossier racine du shell (dans le projet)
        base = Path(__file__).parent.parent
        self.root = base / root_name
        self.root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

        # Crée les dossiers de base au premier lancement
        self._init_default_dirs()

        # Position actuelle
        self.cwd = self.root

    def _init_default_dirs(self):
        """Crée les dossiers par défaut."""
        defaults = [
            "Documents",
            "Downloads",
            "Scripts",
            "Reports",
            ".config",
        ]
        for d in defaults:
            (self.root / d).mkdir(exist_ok=True)

        # Fichier de bienvenue
        welcome = self.root / "README.txt"
        if not welcome.exists():
            welcome.write_text(
                "Welcome to DED_SEC Shell!\n"
                "Type 'help' to see available commands.\n",
                encoding="utf-8"
            )

    def get_cwd(self) -> Path:
        """Retourne le dossier courant."""
        return self.cwd

    def get_cwd_display(self) -> str:
        """Retourne le chemin courant à afficher (style Linux)."""
        try:
            rel = self.cwd.relative_to(self.root)
            if str(rel) == ".":
                return "~"
            return "~/" + str(rel).replace("\\", "/")
        except Exception:
            return "~"

    def resolve_path(self, path: str) -> Path:
        """
        Résout un chemin donné par l'utilisateur.
        Empêche de sortir du sandbox.
        """
        path = path.strip()

        # Home
        if path == "~" or path == "":
            return self.root

        # Chemin absolu virtuel
        if path.startswith("/"):
            target = self.root / path.lstrip("/")
        elif path.startswith("~/"):
            target = self.root / path[2:]
        else:
            # Chemin relatif
            target = self.cwd / path

        # Résout les .. et .
        try:
            target = target.resolve()
        except Exception:
            return self.cwd

        # SÉCURITÉ : empêche de sortir du sandbox
        try:
            target.relative_to(self.root.resolve())
        except ValueError:
            # Tentative de sortie → retour au home
            return self.root

        return target

    def change_dir(self, path: str) -> bool:
        """Change de dossier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)

        if not target.exists():
            return False

        if not target.is_dir():
            return False

        self.cwd = target
        return True

    def list_dir(self, path: str = None) -> list:
        """Liste le contenu d'un dossier."""
        if path is None:
            target = self.cwd
        else:
            target = self.resolve_path(path)

        if not target.exists() or not target.is_dir():
            return []

        items = []
        for item in sorted(target.iterdir()):
            items.append({
                "name": item.name,
                "is_dir": item.is_dir(),
                "size": item.stat().st_size if item.is_file() else 0,
            })
        return items

    def read_file(self, path: str) -> str:
        """Lit le contenu d'un fichier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)
        if not target.exists() or not target.is_file():
            return None
        try:
            return target.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] {e}"

    def write_file(self, path: str, content: str) -> bool:
        """Écrit dans un fichier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)
        try:
            target.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    def make_dir(self, path: str) -> bool:
        """Crée un dossier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)
        try:
            target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    def remove(self, path: str) -> bool:
        """Supprime un fichier ou dossier."""
        target = self.resolve_path(path)

        # SÉCURITÉ : ne pas supprimer le home
        if target == self.root:
            return False

        try:
            if target.is_file():
                target.unlink()
            elif target.is_dir():
                import shutil
                shutil.rmtree(target)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    def exists(self, path: str) -> bool:
        """Vérifie si un chemin existe."""
        return self.resolve_path(path).exists()
3. shell/commands/base.py
Python

# ==========================
# shell/commands/base.py
# Classe de base pour toutes les commandes
# ==========================


class Command:
    """
    Classe de base pour toutes les commandes du shell.
    Chaque commande doit hériter de cette classe.
    """

    name = "unknown"
    description = "No description"
    usage = ""
    category = "misc"

    def __init__(self, shell):
        """
        shell = référence au shell principal
        (donne accès à sandbox, prompt, etc.)
        """
        self.shell = shell
        self.sandbox = shell.sandbox
        self.prompt = shell.prompt

    def execute(self, args: list) -> int:
        """
        Exécute la commande.
        args = liste des arguments (sans le nom de la commande)

        Retourne un code de sortie :
        0  = succès
        1  = erreur
        99 = commande spéciale (ex: exit)
        """
        raise NotImplementedError(
            f"Command {self.name} doit implémenter execute()"
        )

    def help(self) -> str:
        """Retourne l'aide de la commande."""
        return (
            f"{self.name} - {self.description}\n"
            f"Usage: {self.usage}"
        )
